namespace Infragistics.Win.UltraWinStatusBar

	public class UltraStatusBarRole : ComponentRole
	{
		private UltraStatusBar statusBar;

		protected override ISynchronizeInvoke SynchronizingObject => (ISynchronizeInvoke)statusBar;

		public UltraStatusBarRole(UltraStatusBar statusBar)
			: base((Component)(object)statusBar, "UltraStatusBar")
		{
			this.statusBar = statusBar;
			((ComponentRole)this).PropertiesCacheCount = 19;
		}

		protected override string[] GetRoleNames()
		{
			return StyleUtils.GetRoleNames();
		}

		protected override void OnStyleChanged()
		{
			statusBar.OnAppStyleChanged();
		}
	}

		internal static object CacheBorderStylePropertyValue(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CachedProperty prop, Role role, UIElementBorderStyle controlVal, UIElementBorderStyle resolveVal)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0010: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UIElementBorderStyle roleBorderStyle = GetRoleBorderStyle(statusBar, role);
			return CachePropertyValue(statusBar, prop, roleBorderStyle, controlVal, (object)(UIElementBorderStyle)0, resolveVal);
		}

		internal static object CacheButtonStylePropertyValue(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CachedProperty prop, Role role, UIElementButtonStyle controlVal, UIElementButtonStyle resolveVal)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0012: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UIElementButtonStyle buttonStyle = GetRole(statusBar, role).GetButtonStyle(((UltraControlBase)statusBar).ComponentRole);
			return CachePropertyValue(statusBar, prop, buttonStyle, controlVal, (object)(UIElementButtonStyle)0, resolveVal);
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CachedProperty prop, object styleValue, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			ComponentRole val = ((statusBar != null) ? ((UltraControlBase)statusBar).ComponentRole : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				return val.CachePropertyValue(styleValue, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal, (int)prop);
			}
			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		private static object ResolveValueHelper(object val, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			if (defVal != resolveVal && object.Equals(val, defVal))
			{
				return resolveVal;
			}
			return val;
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CustomProperty prop, object styleValue, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			ComponentRole val = ((statusBar != null) ? ((UltraControlBase)statusBar).ComponentRole : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				return val.CachePropertyValue(styleValue, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal, (int)prop);
			}
			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		internal static object CachePropertyValue(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CustomProperty prop, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			ComponentRole val = ((statusBar != null) ? ((UltraControlBase)statusBar).ComponentRole : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				string customPropertyName = GetCustomPropertyName(prop);
				object obj = null;
				if (val.HasProperty(customPropertyName))
				{
					obj = val.GetProperty(customPropertyName);
				}
				return val.CachePropertyValue(obj, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal, (int)prop);
			}
			return ResolveValueHelper(controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
		}

		[Conditional("DEBUG")]
		internal static void OutputUnsetCachedProps(ComponentRole role, int indexBeingSet)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			object obj = default(object);
			for (int i = 0; i < role.PropertiesCacheCount; i++)
			{
				if (i != indexBeingSet && !role.GetCachedProperty(i, ref obj))
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(",");
					}
					object value = ((i <= 1) ? ((object)(CustomProperty)i) : ((object)(CachedProperty)i));
					stringBuilder.Append(value);
				}
			}
			_ = stringBuilder.Length;
			_ = 0;
		}

		internal static void DirtyCachedPropertyVals(UltraStatusBar statusBar)
		{
			ComponentRole val = ((statusBar != null) ? ((UltraControlBase)statusBar).ComponentRole : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				val.ClearCachedPropertyValues();
			}
		}

		internal static bool GetCachedPropertyVal(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CustomProperty prop, out object val)
		{
			return GetCachedPropertyVal(statusBar, (int)prop, out val);
		}

		internal static bool GetCachedPropertyVal(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CachedProperty prop, out object val)
		{
			return GetCachedPropertyVal(statusBar, (int)prop, out val);
		}

		private static bool GetCachedPropertyVal(UltraStatusBar statusBar, int prop, out object val)
		{
			ComponentRole val2 = ((statusBar != null) ? ((UltraControlBase)statusBar).ComponentRole : null);
			if (val2 != null)
			{
				return val2.GetCachedProperty(prop, ref val);
			}
			val = null;
			return false;
		}

		internal static object GetCachedPropertyVal(UltraStatusBar statusBar, CustomProperty prop, object controlVal, object defVal, object resolveVal)
		{
			if (!GetCachedPropertyVal(statusBar, prop, out var val))
			{
				return CachePropertyValue(statusBar, prop, controlVal, defVal, resolveVal);
			}
			return val;
		}

		internal static string GetCustomPropertyName(CustomProperty prop)
		{
			object[] customPropertiesInfo = GetCustomPropertiesInfo();
			return (string)customPropertiesInfo[(int)prop * 6];
		}

		private static object[] GetCustomPropertiesInfo()
		{
			return new object[12]
			{
				"SizeGripVisible",
				typeof(DefaultableBoolean),
				(object)(DefaultableBoolean)0,
				null,
				null,
				"LD_UltraStatusBar_P_SizeGripVisible",
				"ViewStyle",
				typeof(ViewStyle),
				ViewStyle.Default,
				null,
				null,
				"LD_UltraStatusBar_P_ViewStyle"
			};
		}

		internal static UIElementBorderStyle GetRoleBorderStyle(UltraStatusBar statusBar, Role eRole)
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UIRole role = GetRole(statusBar, eRole);
			if (role == null)
			{
				return (UIElementBorderStyle)0;
			}
			return role.BorderStyle;
		}

		internal static UIElementButtonStyle GetRoleButtonStyle(UltraStatusBar statusBar, Role eRole)
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UIRole role = GetRole(statusBar, eRole);
			if (role == null)
			{
				return (UIElementButtonStyle)0;
			}
			return role.GetButtonStyle((ComponentRole)null);
		}

		internal static UIRole GetRole(UltraStatusPanel panel, Role role)
		{
			ResolutionOrderInfo resOrder;
			return GetRole(panel, role, out resOrder);
		}

		internal static UIRole GetRole(UltraStatusPanel panel, Role role, out ResolutionOrderInfo resOrder)
		{
			return GetRole(panel.UltraStatusBar, role, out resOrder);
		}

		internal static UIRole GetRole(UltraStatusBar statusBar, Role role)
		{
			ResolutionOrderInfo resOrder;
			return GetRole(statusBar, role, out resOrder);
		}

		internal static UIRole GetRole(UltraStatusBar statusBar, Role role, out ResolutionOrderInfo resOrder)
		{
			//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ComponentRole componentRole = ((UltraControlBase)statusBar).ComponentRole;
			resOrder = componentRole.GetResolutionOrderInfo();
			return componentRole.GetRole((int)role);
		}

		internal static string[] GetRoleNames()
		{
			object[] roleInfo = GetRoleInfo();
			string[] array = new string[roleInfo.Length / 5];
			for (int i = 0; i < roleInfo.Length; i += 5)
			{
				string text = (string)roleInfo[i + 1];
				array[i / 5] = text;
			}
			return array;
		}

		internal static AppStyleProperty[] GetCustomProperties()
		{
			//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0044: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0055: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			object[] customPropertiesInfo = GetCustomPropertiesInfo();
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			for (int i = 0; i < customPropertiesInfo.Length; i += 6)
			{
				string text = (string)customPropertiesInfo[i];
				Type type = (Type)customPropertiesInfo[1 + i];
				object defaultValue = customPropertiesInfo[2 + i];
				AppStylePropertyConverter val = (AppStylePropertyConverter)customPropertiesInfo[3 + i];
				ValueConstraint constraint = (ValueConstraint)customPropertiesInfo[4 + i];
				string name = (string)customPropertiesInfo[5 + i];
				AppStyleProperty val2 = new AppStyleProperty(text, type, val);
				val2.DefaultValue = defaultValue;
				val2.Constraint = constraint;
				val2.Description = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(name);
				arrayList.Add(val2);
			}
			return (AppStyleProperty[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(AppStyleProperty));
		}

		internal static UIRoleDefinition[] GetRoleDefinitions()
		{
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0047: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0052: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0059: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0071: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0073: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			object[] roleInfo = GetRoleInfo();
			for (int i = 0; i < roleInfo.Length; i += 5)
			{
				Role role = (Role)roleInfo[i];
				string text = (string)roleInfo[i + 1];
				string text2 = (string)roleInfo[i + 2];
				RoleState[] array = (RoleState[])roleInfo[i + 3];
				SupportedRoleProperties val = (SupportedRoleProperties)roleInfo[i + 4];
				RoleState val2 = (RoleState)0;
				for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
				{
					val2 |= array[j];
				}
				arrayList.Add((object)new UIRoleDefinition(text, text2, val2, val));
			}
			return (UIRoleDefinition[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(UIRoleDefinition));
		}

		internal static object GetViewStyle(ISupportAppStyling stylableObj, CustomProperty viewStyleProp)
		{
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005c: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			ComponentRole componentRole = stylableObj.ComponentRole;
			object obj = null;
			if (componentRole != null)
			{
				string customPropertyName = GetCustomPropertyName(viewStyleProp);
				if (componentRole.HasProperty(customPropertyName))
				{
					obj = componentRole.GetProperty(customPropertyName);
				}
				if (obj == null || object.Equals(obj, ViewStyle.Default))
				{
					ViewStyle viewStyle = componentRole.ViewStyle;
					switch (viewStyle - 1)
					{
					case 5:
						obj = ViewStyle.Office2007;
						break;
					case 3:
						obj = ViewStyle.Office2003;
						break;
					case 4:
						obj = ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005;
						break;
					case 0:
						obj = ViewStyle.Standard;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return obj;
		}
	}
	public class UltraWinStatusBarAssemblyStyleInfo : AssemblyStyleInfo
	{
		public override UIRoleDefinition[] GetRoles()
		{
			return StyleUtils.GetRoleDefinitions();
		}

		public override ComponentRoleDefinition[] GetComponents()
		{
			//IL_000f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0015: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ComponentRoleDefinition val = new ComponentRoleDefinition("UltraStatusBar", typeof(UltraStatusBar));
			val.RelatedRoles = StyleUtils.GetRoleNames();
			val.UsesToolTips = true;
			val.CustomProperties = StyleUtils.GetCustomProperties();
			val.SupportedProperties = (SupportedComponentProperties)60;
			return (ComponentRoleDefinition[])(object)new ComponentRoleDefinition[1] { val };
		}
	}

		public void InitializeDialog(RichTextBox richText)
		{
			this.richText = richText;
			int num = 1;
			int lineCount = UltraStatusPanel.GetLineCount(this.richText);
			string format = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("CaretPositionDialogLineNumberCaption");
			labelDescription.Text = string.Format(format, num, lineCount);
			if (this.richText == null)
			{
				textLineNumber.Text = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = 1;
				int num3 = richText.SelectionStart + richText.SelectionLength;
				if (num3 > 0)
				{
					num2 = richText.GetLineFromCharIndex(num3) + 1;
				}
				textLineNumber.Text = num2.ToString();
			}
			textLineNumber.SelectAll();
		}

		private void buttonOk_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			string text = null;
			if (richText == null)
			{
				text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("CaretPositionDialogUnableToNavigate");
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				int val = int.Parse(textLineNumber.Text);
				val = Math.Max(1, Math.Min(val, NativeWindowMethods.GetLines(richText).Length));
				val--;
				int characterIndexFromLine = GetCharacterIndexFromLine(val);
				richText.Select(characterIndexFromLine, 0);
				try
				{
					UIPermission uIPermission = null;
					if (DisposableObject.HasSamePublicKey(GetType()))
					{
						uIPermission = new UIPermission(UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows);
						if (Utilities.CanAssert)
						{
							uIPermission.Assert();
						}
					}
					richText.Focus();
					if (uIPermission != null)
					{
						CodeAccessPermission.RevertAssert();
					}
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
				richText.ScrollToCaret();
				Close();
				return;
			}
			catch (FormatException)
			{
				text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("CaretPositionDialogMustEnterNumeric");
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				text = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("CaretPositionDialogUnableToNavigate");
			}
			UltraMessageBoxManager.Show((IWin32Window)this, text, Application.ProductName, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
		}

		private void buttonCancel_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Close();
		}

		private int GetCharacterIndexFromLine(int lineNumber)
		{
			int num = -1;
			try
			{
				SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					securityPermission.Assert();
				}
				return NativeWindowMethods.GetLineIndex(lineNumber, richText);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				return num;
			}
			num = 0;
			string[] lines = NativeWindowMethods.GetLines(richText);
			for (int i = 0; i < lineNumber; i++)
			{
				num += lines[i].Length + 1;
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	[ToolboxItem(false)]
	public class NumericTextBox : TextBox
	{
		private const int ES_NUMBER = 8192;

		protected override CreateParams CreateParams
		{
			[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode)]
			[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.InheritanceDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode)]
			get
			{
				CreateParams createParams = base.CreateParams;
				createParams.Style |= 8192;
				return createParams;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ClockUIElement : UIElement
	{
		private DateTime time = DateTime.Now;

		private const int DEFAULT_SIZE = 16;

		public DateTime Time
		{
			get
			{
				return time;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == time))
				{
					time = value;
					if (!((UIElement)this).IsDrawing)
					{
						((UIElement)this).Invalidate();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public ClockUIElement(bool autoUpdate, UIElement parent)
			: base(parent)
		{
			if (autoUpdate)
			{
				TimerManager.TimeElapsed += OnTimeElapsed;
			}
		}

		private void OnTimeElapsed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			time = DateTime.Now;
			if (!((UIElement)this).IsDrawing && !((DisposableObject)this).Disposed && !((DisposableObject)(object)((UIElement)this).ControlElement).Disposed)
			{
				((UIElement)this).Invalidate();
			}
		}

		protected override void DrawImage(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			DrawClockImage(ref drawParams, time);
		}

		public void DrawClockImage(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams, DateTime time)
		{
			Rectangle rectInsideBorders = ((UIElement)this).RectInsideBorders;
			Point location = rectInsideBorders.Location;
			int num = Math.Min(rectInsideBorders.Width, rectInsideBorders.Height);
			double num2 = Math.Max(num / 16, 1);
			Color color = ((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).GetBackColor(((UIElement)this).Enabled);
			Color color2 = ((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).GetForeColor(((UIElement)this).Enabled);
			if (color.Equals(Color.Transparent))
			{
				color = SystemColors.Window;
			}
			if (color2.Equals(Color.Transparent))
			{
				color2 = SystemColors.WindowText;
			}
			SolidBrush solidBrush = null;
			SolidBrush solidBrush2 = null;
			Pen pen = null;
			Graphics graphics = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics;
			double num3 = (double)num / 2.0 - (double)((num + 1) % 2) / 2.0;
			if (time.Hour < 12)
			{
				solidBrush = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).CreateSolidBrush(color);
				pen = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)0, color2);
			}
			else
			{
				solidBrush = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).CreateSolidBrush(color2);
				pen = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetPen((PenType)0, color);
			}
			PixelOffsetMode pixelOffsetMode = graphics.PixelOffsetMode;
			graphics.PixelOffsetMode = PixelOffsetMode.HighQuality;
			graphics.FillEllipse(solidBrush, location.X, location.Y, (int)(num3 * 2.0), (int)(num3 * 2.0));
			graphics.DrawEllipse(pen, location.X, location.Y, (int)(num3 * 2.0), (int)(num3 * 2.0));
			int num5;
			int num4 = (num5 = (int)num3);
			if (num2 <= 1.5)
			{
				solidBrush2 = new SolidBrush(pen.Color);
			}
			double num6;
			int num7;
			int num8;
			for (int i = 1; i <= 12; i++)
			{
				num6 = (double)i / 6.0;
				num7 = (int)(num3 * Math.Sin(num6 * Math.PI));
				num8 = (int)(num3 * Math.Cos(num6 * Math.PI));
				if (num2 > 1.5)
				{
					int num9 = (int)((num3 - num2) * Math.Sin(num6 * Math.PI));
					int num10 = (int)((num3 - num2) * Math.Cos(num6 * Math.PI));
					graphics.DrawLine(pen, num4 + num9 + location.X, num5 - num10 + location.Y, num4 + num7 + location.X, num5 - num8 + location.Y);
				}
				else
				{
					int x = num4 + num7 + location.X;
					int y = num5 - num8 + location.Y;
					graphics.FillRectangle(solidBrush2, x, y, 1, 1);
				}
			}
			num3 -= 1.0;
			num6 = (double)time.Minute / 30.0;
			if (num2 < 1.5)
			{
				num4 = ++num5;
			}
			num7 = (int)(num3 * Math.Sin(num6 * Math.PI));
			num8 = (int)(num3 * Math.Cos(num6 * Math.PI));
			graphics.DrawLine(pen, num4 + location.X, num5 + location.Y, num4 + num7 + location.X, num5 - num8 + location.Y);
			num6 = (double)time.Hour / 6.0;
			num7 = (int)(num3 * 0.7 * Math.Sin(num6 * Math.PI));
			num8 = (int)(num3 * 0.7 * Math.Cos(num6 * Math.PI));
			graphics.DrawLine(pen, num4 + location.X, num5 + location.Y, num4 + num7 + location.X, num5 - num8 + location.Y);
			solidBrush.Dispose();
			solidBrush2?.Dispose();
			graphics.PixelOffsetMode = pixelOffsetMode;
		}
	}

						((UIElement)this).Invalidate();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void RefreshIcon()
		{
			if (image != null)
			{
				image.Dispose();
				image = null;
				if (stream != null)
				{
					stream.Close();
					stream = null;
				}
			}
			Form form = this.form;
			if (form != null && form.Icon != null)
			{
				image = this.form.Icon.ToBitmap();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCheckStateChange()
		{
			try
			{
				if (form != null && form.MdiParent != null)
				{
					if (form == form.MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild)
					{
						((ButtonUIElementBase)this).InitializeCheckState(true);
					}
					else if (form.WindowState == FormWindowState.Minimized)
					{
						FormWindowState windowState = ((form.MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild != null && form.MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild.WindowState != FormWindowState.Minimized) ? form.MdiParent.ActiveMdiChild.WindowState : FormWindowState.Normal);
						form.WindowState = windowState;
					}
					else
					{
						form.Activate();
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			((ButtonUIElementBase)this).OnCheckStateChange();
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			if (form != null)
			{
				form.TextChanged -= TextChangedHandler;
			}
			if (form != null)
			{
				form = null;
			}
			if (image != null)
			{
				image.Dispose();
				image = null;
			}
			((UIElement)this).OnDispose();
		}

		private void OnFormTextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!((UIElement)this).IsDrawing)
			{
				((UIElement)this).Invalidate();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseHover()
		{
			((UIElement)this).OnMouseHover();
			if (((ImageAndTextButtonUIElementBase)this).ImageAndTextElement != null)
			{
				TextUIElementBase textElement = (TextUIElementBase)(object)((ImageAndTextButtonUIElementBase)this).ImageAndTextElement.TextElement;
				if (textElement != null && textElement.Text != null && textElement.CharactersRendered < textElement.Text.Length)
				{
					UltraStatusPanel panel = ((UIElement)this).GetContext(typeof(UltraStatusPanel), true) as UltraStatusPanel;
					UltraStatusBar.DisplayMDIPanelToolTip(panel, ((TextUIElementBase)((ImageAndTextButtonUIElementBase)this).ImageAndTextElement.TextElement).Text);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave()
		{
			UltraStatusBar.HidePanelToolTip();
			((ButtonUIElementBase)this).OnMouseLeave();
		}
	}

		private void AddMDIButtons(UIElementsCollection oldElements, Rectangle workRect)
		{
			if (workRect.Width <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			Form form = null;
			UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
			form = ((panel == null || !(panel.Control is Form)) ? UltraStatusBar.ParentForm : (panel.Control as Form));
			if (form == null || !form.IsMdiContainer)
			{
				return;
			}
			Form[] mdiChildren = form.MdiChildren;
			if (mdiChildren == null || mdiChildren.Length < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			Form[] array = mdiChildren;
			foreach (Form form2 in array)
			{
				if (form2.Visible)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (num < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num2 = workRect.Width / num;
			int num3 = ((num2 == 0) ? workRect.Width : (workRect.Width % (num2 * num)));
			if (num2 > 100)
			{
				num2 = 100;
				num3 = 0;
			}
			Form activeMdiChild = form.ActiveMdiChild;
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			Form[] array2 = mdiChildren;
			foreach (Form form3 in array2)
			{
				if (form3.Visible)
				{
					MDIButtonUIElement mDIButtonUIElement = UIElement.ExtractExistingElement((ArrayList)(object)oldElements, typeof(MDIButtonUIElement), true) as MDIButtonUIElement;
					if (mDIButtonUIElement == null)
					{
						mDIButtonUIElement = new MDIButtonUIElement(activeMdiChild == form3, (IUIElementImageAndTextProvider)(object)ultraStatusBar, (UIElement)(object)this);
					}
					else
					{
						((ButtonUIElementBase)mDIButtonUIElement).InitializeCheckState(activeMdiChild == form3);
					}
					mDIButtonUIElement.Form = form3;
					((TextUIElementBase)((ImageAndTextButtonUIElementBase)mDIButtonUIElement).ImageAndTextElement.TextElement).TrackCharactersRendered = true;
					if (num3 > 0)
					{
						((UIElement)mDIButtonUIElement).Rect = new Rectangle(workRect.Left, workRect.Top, num2 + 1, workRect.Height);
						num3--;
						workRect.X += num2 + 1;
						workRect.Width -= num2 + 1;
					}
					else
					{
						((UIElement)mDIButtonUIElement).Rect = new Rectangle(workRect.Left, workRect.Top, num2, workRect.Height);
						workRect.X += num2;
						workRect.Width -= num2;
					}
					((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)mDIButtonUIElement);
					if (workRect.Width <= 0)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseHover()
		{
			((UIElement)this).OnMouseHover();
			UltraStatusBar.DisplayPanelToolTip(Panel);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave()
		{

		}

		protected override void OnDoubleClick(bool adjustableArea)
		{
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
			if (ultraStatusBar == null || panel == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (adjustableArea && ((UIElement)this).Adjustable)
			{
				CancelablePanelEventArgs e = new CancelablePanelEventArgs(panel);
				ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.BeforeAutoSizePanel, e);
				if (!e.Cancel)
				{
					panel.Width = panel.AutoSizeWidth;
					ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.AfterAutoSizePanel, new PanelEventArgs(panel));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				panel.ShowCaretPositionDialog();
			}
			if (lastMouseDown != null)
			{
				ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.PanelDoubleClick, new PanelClickEventArgs(panel, lastMouseDown.Button, lastMouseDown.Clicks, lastMouseDown.X, lastMouseDown.Y));
			}
		}

		protected override bool CanStartAdjustment(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
			if (ultraStatusBar == null || panel == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			CancelablePanelEventArgs e2 = new CancelablePanelEventArgs(panel);
			ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.BeforeDragResize, e2);
			return !e2.Cancel;
		}

		public override void GetAdjustmentRange(Point point, out UIElementAdjustmentRangeParams range)
		{
			((AdjustableUIElement)this).GetAdjustmentRange(point, ref range);
			range.leftRightAdjustmentBar.Width = UltraStatusBar.InterPanelSpacing;
			range.maxDeltaLeft = -(((UIElement)this).Rect.Width - Panel.MinWidth);
			range.maxDeltaRight -= range.leftRightAdjustmentBar.Width;
		}

		public override void ApplyAdjustment(Point delta)
		{
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
			if (ultraStatusBar != null && panel != null)
			{
				if (ultraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.Immediate)
				{
					panel.Width += delta.X;
				}
				PanelEventArgs e = new PanelEventArgs(panel);
				ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.AfterDragResize, e);
				if (ultraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.Immediate)
				{
					((AdjustableUIElement)this).ApplyAdjustment(delta);
				}
			}
		}

		public override Cursor GetAdjustableCursor(Point point)
		{
			return Cursors.VSplit;
		}

		protected override void OnNewDeltaX(int newDeltaX)
		{
			((AdjustableUIElement)this).OnNewDeltaX(newDeltaX);
			if (UltraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.Deferred && newDeltaX != lastDelta)
			{
				if (lastDelta == -1)
				{
					Panel.Width += newDeltaX;
				}
				else
				{
					Panel.Width += newDeltaX - lastDelta;
				}
				lastDelta = newDeltaX;
				((UIElement)this).Parent.DirtyChildElements();
				((UIElement)this).Control.Update();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnElementAdjustmentStart(bool isUpDownAdjustment, bool initialUpDownAdjustmentPointInBottomBorder, bool isLeftRightAdjustment, bool initialLeftRightAdjustmentPointInRightBorder)
		{
			lastDelta = -1;
			((AdjustableUIElement)this).OnElementAdjustmentStart(isUpDownAdjustment, initialUpDownAdjustmentPointInBottomBorder, isLeftRightAdjustment, initialLeftRightAdjustmentPointInRightBorder);
		}

		protected override bool OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e, bool adjustableArea, ref UIElement captureMouseForElement)
		{
			lastMouseDown = e;
			return ((AdjustableUIElement)this).OnMouseDown(e, adjustableArea, ref captureMouseForElement);
		}

		protected override void OnClick()
		{
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			if (ultraStatusBar != null && lastMouseDown != null)
			{
				ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.PanelClick, new PanelClickEventArgs(Panel, lastMouseDown.Button, lastMouseDown.Clicks, lastMouseDown.X, lastMouseDown.Y));
			}
			((UIElement)this).OnClick();
		}
	}

			private UltraStatusPanel GetRelatedVisiblePanel(UltraStatusPanel startingPanel, bool forward)
			{
				UltraStatusPanel ultraStatusPanel = null;
				int num = startingPanel?.Index ?? (forward ? (-1) : Panels.Count);
				int num2 = (forward ? 1 : (-1));
				for (int i = num + num2; i >= 0 && i < Panels.Count; i += num2)
				{
					ultraStatusPanel = Panels[i];
					if (ultraStatusPanel.Visible)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (ultraStatusPanel != null)
				{
					return ultraStatusPanel;
				}
				return null;
			}

		bool IList.IsFixedSize => false;

		object IList.this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return GetItem(index);
			}
			set
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException();
				}
				if (index < 0 || index > Count - 1)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
				}
				if (!(value is UltraStatusPanel))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_17"));
				}
				UltraStatusPanel ultraStatusPanel = (UltraStatusPanel)value;
				if (Contains(ultraStatusPanel))
				{
					if (IndexOf(ultraStatusPanel) == index)
					{
						return;
					}
					Remove(ultraStatusPanel);
				}
				AddHelper(ultraStatusPanel, notify: true, index);
			}
		}

		public UltraStatusPanel this[int index] => GetItem(index) as UltraStatusPanel;

		public UltraStatusPanel Add(string key, string text, PanelStyle style)
		{
			ValidateKeyDoesNotExist(key);
			UltraStatusPanel ultraStatusPanel = new UltraStatusPanel(key, text, style, this);
			AddHelper(ultraStatusPanel, notify: true);
			return ultraStatusPanel;
		}

		public int Add(object value)
		{
			return AddHelper(value as UltraStatusPanel, notify: true);
		}

		protected int AddHelper(UltraStatusPanel panel, bool notify)
		{
			int count = Count;
			AddHelper(panel, notify, count);
			return count;
		}

		protected int AddHelper(UltraStatusPanel panel, bool notify, int newIndex)
		{
			if (panel == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_18"));
			}
			if (newIndex < 0 || newIndex > Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
			}
			if (Contains(panel))
			{
				return IndexOf(panel);
			}
			string value = panel?.Key;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) && owningControl != null && owningControl.Initializing)
			{
				int num = IndexOf(panel.Key);
				if (num >= 0 && this[num].Inherited)
				{
					return num;
				}
			}
			if (panel.UltraStatusBar != null && panel.UltraStatusBar != owningControl)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_19"));
			}
			ValidateKeyDoesNotExist(panel.Key);
			InternalInsert(newIndex, panel);
			panel.SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			panel.InitControl(owningControl);
			if (notify)
			{
				owningControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder);
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Panel);
			}
			return newIndex;
		}

		public void Insert(int index, UltraStatusPanel panel)
		{
			AddHelper(panel, notify: true, index);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			PanelsEnumerator panelsEnumerator = GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				while (panelsEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					UltraStatusPanel current = panelsEnumerator.Current;
					current.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					current.UnparentCustomControl();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				IDisposable disposable = panelsEnumerator as IDisposable;
				if (disposable != null)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
			InternalClear();
			owningControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder);
			NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Panel);
		}

		public void Remove(object obj)
		{
			UltraStatusPanel panel = obj as UltraStatusPanel;
			Remove(panel);
		}

		public void Remove(UltraStatusPanel panel)
		{
			if (Contains(panel))
			{
				if (panel == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_20"));
				}
				panel.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				panel.UnparentCustomControl();
				InternalRemove(panel);
				owningControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder);
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Panel);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			UltraStatusPanel ultraStatusPanel = this[index];
			if (ultraStatusPanel == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
			}
			ultraStatusPanel.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
			InternalRemoveAt(index);
			NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Panel);
		}

		void IList.Insert(int index, object obj)
		{
			AddHelper(obj as UltraStatusPanel, notify: true, index);
		}

		public void AddRange(UltraStatusPanel[] panels)
		{
			if (panels == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			foreach (UltraStatusPanel ultraStatusPanel in panels)
			{
				if (ultraStatusPanel != null && !ultraStatusPanel.Disposed)
				{
					AddHelper(ultraStatusPanel, notify: false);
				}
			}
			owningControl.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder);
			NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Panel);
		}

		public PanelsEnumerator GetEnumerator()
		{
			return new PanelsEnumerator(this);
		}

		bool IList.Contains(object value)
		{
			return Contains(value);
		}
	}

			private List<MDIButtonUIElement> GetMdiButtonUIElements()
			{
				List<MDIButtonUIElement> list = new List<MDIButtonUIElement>();
				if (Panel.Style == PanelStyle.MDIList)
				{
					PanelUIElement panelUIElement = PanelUIElement;
					if (panelUIElement != null)
					{
						UIElementEnumerator enumerator = ((UIElement)panelUIElement).ChildElements.GetEnumerator();
						try
						{
							while (enumerator.MoveNext())
							{
								UIElement current = enumerator.Current;
								if (current is MDIButtonUIElement item)
								{
									list.Add(item);
								}
							}
						}
						finally
						{
							if (enumerator is IDisposable disposable)
							{
								disposable.Dispose();
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return list;
			}

					if (control != null && !control.Disposing && !control.IsDisposed)

						return control.RectangleToScreen(rect);
					}
				}
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}

			public override ControlType GetControlType()

				case PanelStyle.ControlContainer:

				case PanelStyle.MDIList:
					return ControlType.Group;
				case PanelStyle.Progress:

			public override object GetPatternProvider(AutomationPatternIds patternId)
			{
				//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0006: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
				//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if ((int)patternId == 10015)
				{
					PanelStyle style = Panel.Style;
					if (style == PanelStyle.StateButton)

				return ((FragmentUIAProviderStub)this).GetPatternProvider(patternId);

			public override IRawElementProviderFragment NavigateChildren(FragmentUIAProviderStub childStub, bool forward)
			{
				if (Panel.Style == PanelStyle.MDIList && childStub is MDIButtonUIElement.MDIButtonUIElementUiaProviderStub mDIButtonUIElementUiaProviderStub)
				{
					List<MDIButtonUIElement> mdiButtonUIElements = GetMdiButtonUIElements();
					int num = mdiButtonUIElements.IndexOf(mDIButtonUIElementUiaProviderStub.MDIButtonUIElement);
					num += (forward ? 1 : (-1));
					if (num >= 0 && num < mdiButtonUIElements.Count)
					{
						return ((UIElement)mdiButtonUIElements[num]).UIAutomationProvider;
					}
					return null;
				}
				return null;
			}

			void IToggleProvider.Toggle()
			{
				UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
				PanelStyle style = panel.Style;
				if (style == PanelStyle.StateButton)
				{
					Panel.Checked = !Panel.Checked;
				}
			}

		public sealed class UltraStatusPanelTypeConverter : TypeConverter
		{
			public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
			{
				if (destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
			}

			public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
			{
				if (destinationType == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_10"));
				}
				if (destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor) && value is UltraStatusPanel)
				{
					ConstructorInfo constructor = typeof(UltraStatusPanel).GetConstructor(new Type[0]);
					if (constructor != null)
					{
						return new InstanceDescriptor(constructor, new object[0], isComplete: false);
					}
				}
				return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
			}
		}

		public class UltraStatusPanelAccessibleObject : AccessibleObjectWrapper
		{
			private UltraStatusPanel panel;

			public override string DefaultAction => panel.DefaultAction;

			public override Rectangle Bounds
			{
				get
				{
					UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
					Control control = ((uIElement == null) ? null : uIElement.Control);
					if (control != null && !control.IsDisposed && !control.Disposing)
					{
						return control.RectangleToScreen(uIElement.Rect);
					}
					return Rectangle.Empty;
				}
			}

			public override string KeyboardShortcut
			{
				get
				{
					PanelStyle style = panel.Style;
					if (style == PanelStyle.Button || style == PanelStyle.StateButton)
					{
						char firstMnemonicChar = Utilities.GetFirstMnemonicChar(panel.DisplayText);
						if (firstMnemonicChar != 0)
						{
							return new string(firstMnemonicChar, 1);
						}
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			public override string Name
			{
				get
				{
					string text = ((AccessibleObjectWrapper)this).Name;
					if (text == null)
					{
						text = ((panel.AccessibleName == null) ? Utilities.StripMnemonics(panel.DisplayText) : panel.AccessibleName);
					}
					return text;
				}
				set
				{
					((AccessibleObjectWrapper)this).Name = value;
				}
			}

			public override AccessibleObject Parent
			{
				get
				{
					if (panel.UltraStatusBar == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return ((Control)(object)panel.UltraStatusBar).AccessibilityObject;
				}
			}

			public override AccessibleRole Role
			{
				get
				{
					switch (panel.Style)
					{
					case PanelStyle.AutoStatusText:
					case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
					case PanelStyle.CursorPosition:
					case PanelStyle.Date:
					case PanelStyle.KeyState:
					case PanelStyle.Text:
						return AccessibleRole.StaticText;
					case PanelStyle.Marquee:
						return AccessibleRole.StaticText;
					case PanelStyle.Time:
						return AccessibleRole.Clock;
					case PanelStyle.ControlContainer:
						return AccessibleRole.Pane;
					case PanelStyle.MDIList:
						return AccessibleRole.Grouping;
					case PanelStyle.Progress:
						return AccessibleRole.ProgressBar;
					case PanelStyle.Button:
						return AccessibleRole.PushButton;
					case PanelStyle.StateButton:
						return AccessibleRole.CheckButton;
					default:
						return AccessibleRole.None;
					}
				}
			}

			public override AccessibleStates State
			{
				get
				{
					AccessibleStates accessibleStates = AccessibleStates.None;
					UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = panel.UltraStatusBar;
					UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
					switch (panel.Style)
					{
					case PanelStyle.Marquee:
						if (panel.MarqueeInfo.IsActive)
						{
							accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Marqueed;
						}
						break;
					case PanelStyle.StateButton:
						if (panel.Checked)
						{
							accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Checked;
						}
						break;
					}
					if (!panel.Visible || ultraStatusBar == null || !((Control)(object)ultraStatusBar).Visible)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Invisible;
					}
					else if (uIElement == null || uIElement.ClipRect.Size.IsEmpty)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Offscreen;
					}
					if (!panel.Enabled || ultraStatusBar == null || !((Control)(object)ultraStatusBar).Enabled)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Unavailable;
					}
					if (panel.SizingMode == PanelSizingMode.Adjustable && ultraStatusBar != null && ultraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.None)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Sizeable;
					}
					if (uIElement != null && panel.IsButtonStyle && uIElement.GetDescendant(typeof(PanelButtonUIElement)) is PanelButtonUIElement panelButtonUIElement && ((ButtonUIElementBase)panelButtonUIElement).IsMouseDown)
					{
						accessibleStates |= AccessibleStates.Pressed;
					}
					return accessibleStates;
				}
			}

			public override string Value
			{
				get
				{
					PanelStyle style = panel.Style;
					if (style == PanelStyle.Progress)
					{
						return panel.progressBarInfo.Value.ToString();
					}
					return null;
				}
				set
				{
				}
			}

			public UltraStatusPanelAccessibleObject(UltraStatusPanel panel)
			{
				this.panel = panel;
			}

			protected override Control GetMarshallingControl()
			{
				return (Control)(object)panel.UltraStatusBar;
			}

			public override AccessibleObject GetChild(int index)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
				if (uIElement != null && uIElement.HasChildElements)
				{
					int num = 0;
					UIElementEnumerator enumerator = uIElement.ChildElements.GetEnumerator();
					try
					{
						while (enumerator.MoveNext())
						{
							UIElement current = enumerator.Current;
							if (current != null && current.IsAccessibleElement)
							{
								if (num == index)
								{
									return current.AccessibilityInstance;
								}
								num++;
							}
						}
					}
					finally
					{
						if (enumerator is IDisposable disposable)
						{
							disposable.Dispose();
						}
					}
				}
				return ((AccessibleObjectWrapper)this).GetChild(index);
			}

			public override int GetChildCount()
			{
				int num = 0;
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
				if (uIElement != null && uIElement.HasChildElements)
				{
					UIElementEnumerator enumerator = uIElement.ChildElements.GetEnumerator();
					try
					{
						while (enumerator.MoveNext())
						{
							UIElement current = enumerator.Current;
							if (current != null && current.IsAccessibleElement)
							{
								num++;
							}
						}
					}
					finally
					{
						if (enumerator is IDisposable disposable)
						{
							disposable.Dispose();
						}
					}
				}
				return num;
			}

			public override AccessibleObject HitTest(int x, int y)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
				if (uIElement != null)
				{
					return uIElement.HitTest(x, y);
				}
				return null;
			}

			public override AccessibleObject Navigate(AccessibleNavigation navdir)
			{
				switch (navdir)
				{
				case AccessibleNavigation.Up:
				case AccessibleNavigation.Down:
					return null;
				case AccessibleNavigation.Left:
				case AccessibleNavigation.Previous:
				{
					if (panel.UltraStatusBar == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					int index2 = panel.Index;
					if (index2 == 0)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return panel.UltraStatusBar.Panels[index2 - 1].AccessibilityObject;
				}
				case AccessibleNavigation.Right:
				case AccessibleNavigation.Next:
				{
					if (panel.UltraStatusBar == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					int index = panel.Index;
					if (index < panel.UltraStatusBar.Panels.Count - 1)
					{
						return panel.UltraStatusBar.Panels[index + 1].AccessibilityObject;
					}
					if (index == panel.UltraStatusBar.Panels.Count - 1)
					{
						UIElement descendant = ((UIElement)panel.UltraStatusBar.UIElement).GetDescendant(typeof(SizeGripUIElement));
						if (descendant != null)
						{
							return descendant.AccessibilityInstance;
						}
						return null;
					}
					break;
				}
				}
				return ((AccessibleObjectWrapper)this).Navigate(navdir);
			}

			public override void DoDefaultAction()
			{
				panel.DoDefaultAction();
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public int AutoSizeWidth
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00f7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0063: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0087: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_008c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00bf: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_00c4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				if (UltraStatusBar == null || !CanAutoSize)
				{
					return width;
				}
				UpdateAutoSizeInfo();
				AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
				AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)192;
				ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				ContentAlignment contentAlignment = AppearanceData.ContentAlignmentFromHVAlign(((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageHAlign, ((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageVAlign);
				int num = 0;
				if (IsButtonStyle)
				{
					UIElementButtonType val = (UIElementButtonType)(IsStateButtonStyle ? 1 : 0);
					num = ImageAndTextUIElement.CalculateButtonImageAndTextSize(autoSizeImage, autoSizeText, Size.Empty, Size.Empty, Size.Empty, false, UltraStatusBar.ButtonStyleResolved, val, Border3DSide.All, contentAlignment).Width;
					if (!Padding.IsEmpty)
					{
						UIElementBorderWidths val2 = DrawUtility.CalculateBorderWidths(BorderStyleResolved, Border3DSide.All);
						num += ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val2)).Left + ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val2)).Right;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					num = ImageAndTextUIElement.CalculateImageAndTextSize(autoSizeImage, autoSizeText, Size.Empty, Size.Empty, Size.Empty, false, BorderStyleResolved, Border3DSide.All, contentAlignment).Width;
				}
				if (Padding.Width > 0)
				{
					num += Padding.Width * 2;
				}
				if (num < MinWidth)
				{
					return MinWidth;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		private HotkeyPrefix HotKeyPrefix
		{
			get
			{
				PanelStyle panelStyle = Style;
				if (panelStyle == PanelStyle.Button || panelStyle == PanelStyle.StateButton)
				{
					UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
					if (ultraStatusBar != null && ultraStatusBar.UseMnemonic)
					{
						return HotkeyPrefix.Show;
					}
				}
				return HotkeyPrefix.None;
			}
		}

		[Localizable(true)]
		public string Text

			get
			{
				return text;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(text == value) && (value != null || !(text == string.Empty)))
				{
					VerifyCanBeModified();
					if (value == null)
					{
						text = string.Empty;
					}
					else
					{
						text = value;
					}
					DirtyText();
					if (UltraStatusBar != null)
					{
						UltraStatusBar.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.NameChange, Index);
					}
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Text);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public string DisplayText
		{
			get
			{
				switch (style)
				{
				case PanelStyle.Date:
				case PanelStyle.Time:
					return FormatDateTime(DateTime.Now);
				case PanelStyle.ControlContainer:
					return string.Empty;
				case PanelStyle.Progress:
					return UltraProgressBar.FormatText((IProgressBarInfo)(object)ProgressBarInfo, (string)null);
				case PanelStyle.KeyState:
					if (KeyStateInfo.Toggled)
					{
						return KeyStateInfo.OnTextResolved;
					}
					if (KeyStateInfo.DisplayStyle == KeyStateDisplayStyle.ChangeText)
					{
						return KeyStateInfo.OffTextResolved;
					}
					return KeyStateInfo.OnTextResolved;
				case PanelStyle.AutoStatusText:
				case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
				case PanelStyle.CursorPosition:
					if (displayText != null && displayText.Length > 0)
					{
						return displayText;
					}
					return text;
				case PanelStyle.Marquee:
					return MarqueeInfo.CurrentText;
				default:
					return text;
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraStatusPanel_P_Visible")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(true)]
		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return visible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (visible == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				VerifyCanBeModified();
				visible = value;
				if (UltraStatusBar != null)
				{
					if (visible)
					{
						UltraStatusBar.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Show, Index);
					}
					else
					{
						UltraStatusBar.AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Hide, Index);
					}
				}
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Visible);
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraStatusPanel_P_SizingMode")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		[DefaultValue(PanelSizingMode.Fixed)]
		public PanelSizingMode SizingMode
		{
			get
			{
				return sizingMode;
			}
			set
			{
				if (sizingMode != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(PanelSizingMode), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(PanelSizingMode));
					}
					VerifyCanBeModified();
					if (Style == PanelStyle.Marquee)
					{
						DirtyText();
					}
					sizingMode = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.SizingMode);
				}
			}
		}

					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.CharacterPositionFormat);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool IsButtonStyle
		{
			get
			{
				PanelStyle panelStyle = style;
				if (panelStyle == PanelStyle.Button || panelStyle == PanelStyle.MDIList || panelStyle == PanelStyle.StateButton)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool IsStateButtonStyle
		{
			get
			{
				if (Style != PanelStyle.MDIList)
				{
					return Style == PanelStyle.StateButton;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private bool CanAutoSize
		{
			get
			{
				switch (Style)
				{
				case PanelStyle.Progress:
					if (progressBarInfo != null)
					{
						return progressBarInfo.ShowLabel;
					}
					return true;
				case PanelStyle.ControlContainer:
				case PanelStyle.MDIList:
					return false;
				default:
					return true;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual string DefaultAction
		{
			get
			{
				switch (Style)
				{
				case PanelStyle.StateButton:
					if (!Checked)
					{
						return Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("AccessibleActionCheck");
					}
					return Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("AccessibleActionUncheck");
				case PanelStyle.Button:
					return Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("AccessibleActionPush");
				case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
					return Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("AccessibleActionShowCaretPosDialog");
				default:
					return null;
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_Panel_P_NotificationBadgeSettings")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public NotificationBadgeSettings NotificationBadgeSettings
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_0009: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0013: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (notificationBadgeSettings == null)
				{
					notificationBadgeSettings = new NotificationBadgeSettings();
					((SubObjectBase)(object)notificationBadgeSettings).SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				}
				return notificationBadgeSettings;
			}
			set
			{
				if (notificationBadgeSettings != value)
				{
					if (notificationBadgeSettings != null)
					{
						((SubObjectBase)(object)notificationBadgeSettings).SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					}
					notificationBadgeSettings = value;
					if (notificationBadgeSettings != null)
					{
						((SubObjectBase)(object)notificationBadgeSettings).SubObjectPropChanged += base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					}
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.NotificationBadgeSettings);
				}
			}
		}

		internal PanelUiaProviderStub GetUiaProviderStub()
		{
			return GetUiaProviderStub(createIfNeeded: true);
		}

		internal PanelUiaProviderStub GetUiaProviderStub(bool createIfNeeded)
		{
			VerifyPanelUiaProviderStub();
			if (panelUiaProviderStub == null && createIfNeeded)
			{
				panelUiaProviderStub = new PanelUiaProviderStub(this);
			}
			return panelUiaProviderStub;
		}

		private void VerifyPanelUiaProviderStub()
		{
			if (panelUiaProviderStub != null)
			{
				string strA = PanelUiaProviderStub.BuildAutomationId(this);
				if (string.Compare(strA, panelUiaProviderStub.GetAutomationIDInternal()) != 0)
				{
					DisposeUiaProviderStub();
				}
			}
		}

		private void DisposeUiaProviderStub()
		{
			panelUiaProviderStub = null;
		}

		public UltraStatusPanel()
		{
		}

		internal UltraStatusPanel(string key, string text, PanelStyle style, UltraStatusPanelsCollection owningCollection)
		{
			if (owningCollection == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			this.owningCollection = owningCollection;
			owningControl = this.owningCollection.OwningControl;
			Key = key;
			if (text == null)
			{
				this.text = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				this.text = text;
			}
			this.style = style;
		}

		protected internal void InitControl(UltraStatusBar owningControl)
		{
			if (this.owningControl != owningControl && this.owningControl != null)
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_12"));
			}
			this.owningControl = owningControl;
			owningCollection = this.owningControl.Panels;
			ParentCustomControl();
			if (marqueeInfo != null)
			{
				marqueeInfo.ResetMarquee();
			}
			if (keyStateInfo != null)
			{
				keyStateInfo.Toggled = KeyStateManager.GetKeyState(keyStateInfo.Key);
			}
			if (progressBarInfo != null)
			{
				progressBarInfo.InitAppearances(this.owningControl.Appearances);
				progressBarInfo.ComponentRole = ((this.owningControl == null) ? null : ((UltraControlBase)this.owningControl).ComponentRole);
			}
		}

		internal void DirtyText()
		{
			autoSizeText = Size.Empty;
			if (sizingMode == PanelSizingMode.Automatic)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)UIElement;
				if (uIElement != null)
				{
					((UIElement)uIElement.ControlElement).DirtyChildElements(true);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void DirtyImage()
		{
			autoSizeImage = Size.Empty;
			if (sizingMode == PanelSizingMode.Automatic)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)UIElement;
				if (uIElement != null)
				{
					((UIElement)uIElement.ControlElement).DirtyChildElements(true);
				}
			}
		}

		internal void DirtyCachedPropertyValues()
		{
			cachedBorderStyle = null;
		}

		internal void UpdateDisplayText(string newValue)
		{
			if (!(displayText == newValue))
			{
				displayText = newValue;
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)UIElement;
				if (uIElement != null && !uIElement.IsDrawing)
				{
					uIElement.Invalidate();
				}
			}
		}

		internal void SetCustomControlVisibility(bool value)
		{
			if (Style == PanelStyle.ControlContainer && control != null)
			{
				control.Visible = value;
			}
		}

		internal void UnparentCustomControl()
		{
			if (Style == PanelStyle.ControlContainer && control != null && UltraStatusBar != null)
			{
				UltraStatusBar.ReparentControl(control, null);
			}
		}

		internal void ParentCustomControl()
		{
			if (Style == PanelStyle.ControlContainer && UltraStatusBar != null)
			{
				UltraStatusBar.ReparentControl(control, (Control)(object)UltraStatusBar);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (HasAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == appearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				DirtyImage();
				DirtyText();
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Appearance, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (progressBarInfo == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				if (Style == PanelStyle.Progress)
				{
					DirtyText();
					UpdateDisplayText(null);
				}
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.ProgressBarInfo, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (keyStateInfo == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				keyStateInfo.Toggled = KeyStateManager.GetKeyState(keyStateInfo.Key);
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.KeyStateInfo, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (marqueeInfo == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				if (propChangeInfo.PropId is StatusBarPropertyIds)
				{
					StatusBarPropertyIds statusBarPropertyIds = (StatusBarPropertyIds)(object)propChangeInfo.PropId;
					if (statusBarPropertyIds == StatusBarPropertyIds.Direction || statusBarPropertyIds == StatusBarPropertyIds.MarqueeStyle)
					{
						DirtyText();
					}
				}
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.MarqueeInfo, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasNotificationBadgeSettings && propChangeInfo.Source == notificationBadgeSettings)
			{
				DirtyText();
				DirtyImage();
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.NotificationBadgeSettings, propChangeInfo);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChangeInfo);
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			UnhookControl();
			UnparentCustomControl();
			control = null;
			if (HasAppearance)
			{
				appearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				appearanceHolder = null;
			}
			if (progressBarInfo != null)
			{
				progressBarInfo.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				progressBarInfo.Dispose();
				progressBarInfo = null;
			}
			if (keyStateInfo != null)
			{
				keyStateInfo.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				keyStateInfo.Dispose();
				keyStateInfo = null;
			}
			if (notificationBadgeSettings != null)
			{
				((SubObjectBase)(object)notificationBadgeSettings).SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				((DisposableObject)(object)notificationBadgeSettings).Dispose();
				notificationBadgeSettings = null;
			}
			if (notificationBadgeSettingsResolved != null)
			{
				notificationBadgeSettingsResolved.Dispose();
				notificationBadgeSettingsResolved = null;
			}
			base.OnDispose();
		}

		protected virtual void DoDefaultAction()
		{
			if (Enabled && UltraStatusBar != null && ((Control)(object)UltraStatusBar).Enabled)
			{
				switch (Style)
				{
				case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
					ShowCaretPositionDialog();
					break;
				case PanelStyle.Button:
					UltraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.ButtonClick, new PanelEventArgs(this));
					break;
				case PanelStyle.StateButton:
					Checked = !Checked;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void HookControl()
		{
			if ((owningControl != null && owningControl.IsDesignMode) || control == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			control.Disposed += OnControlDisposed;
			switch (style)
			{
			case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
				if (control is RichTextBox)
				{
					RichTextBox richTextBox = control as RichTextBox;
					richTextBox.SelectionChanged += SelectionChangedHandler;
					UpdateSelectionDisplayText(richTextBox);
					richTextBox.ContentsResized += RichTextContentsResizedHandler;
					richTextBox.TextChanged += RichTextChangedHandler;
				}
				break;
			case PanelStyle.CursorPosition:
				if (control != null)
				{
					control.MouseMove += MouseMoveHandler;
					control.MouseLeave += MouseLeaveHandler;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void UnhookControl()
		{
			if ((owningControl != null && owningControl.IsDesignMode) || control == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			control.Disposed -= OnControlDisposed;
			switch (style)
			{
			case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
				if (control is RichTextBox)
				{
					RichTextBox richTextBox = control as RichTextBox;
					richTextBox.ContentsResized -= RichTextContentsResizedHandler;
					richTextBox.TextChanged -= RichTextChangedHandler;
					richTextBox.SelectionChanged -= SelectionChangedHandler;
				}
				break;
			case PanelStyle.CursorPosition:
				if (control != null)
				{
					control.MouseMove -= MouseMoveHandler;
					control.MouseLeave -= MouseLeaveHandler;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void OnRichTextContentsResized(object sender, ContentsResizedEventArgs e)
		{
			RichTextBox richText = sender as RichTextBox;
			UpdateSelectionDisplayText(richText);
		}

		private void OnRichTextChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is RichTextBox { SelectionStart: 0 } richTextBox)
			{
				UpdateSelectionDisplayText(richTextBox);
			}
		}

		private void OnSelectionChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			RichTextBox richText = sender as RichTextBox;
			UpdateSelectionDisplayText(richText);
		}

		private void UpdateSelectionDisplayText(RichTextBox richText)
		{
			if (richText == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				int line = 1;
				int character = 1;
				int selectionStart = richText.SelectionStart;
				string[] lines = NativeWindowMethods.GetLines(richText);
				if (selectionStart > 0)
				{
					line = richText.GetLineFromCharIndex(selectionStart);
					int charIndexFromPosition = NativeWindowMethods.GetCharIndexFromPosition(richText, new Point(0, NativeWindowMethods.GetPositionFromCharIndex(richText, selectionStart).Y));
					character = selectionStart - charIndexFromPosition;
					character = ((character == 0 || lines == null || lines.Length == 0 || (line < lines.Length && lines[line].Length == 0) || (line >= lines.Length && lines[lines.Length - 1].Length == 0 && selectionStart == richText.Text.Length)) ? 1 : (character + 1));
					line++;
				}
				selectionStart++;
				UpdateDisplayText(FormatCharacterPosition(character, line, selectionStart, GetLineCount(richText, lines)));
				DirtyText();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		internal static int GetLineCount(RichTextBox richText)
		{
			return GetLineCount(richText, NativeWindowMethods.GetLines(richText));
		}

		internal static int GetLineCount(RichTextBox richText, string[] lines)
		{
			if (richText == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (lines == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (!richText.WordWrap)
			{
				return lines.Length;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (string text in lines)
			{
				num += text.Length + 1;
			}
			return richText.GetLineFromCharIndex(num) + 1;
		}

		private void OnMouseLeave(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateDisplayText(null);
			DirtyText();
		}

		private void OnMouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			Point pt = new Point(e.X, e.Y);
			if (control != null && control.DisplayRectangle.Contains(pt))
			{
				UpdateDisplayText(FormatCursorPosition(pt.X, pt.Y));
			}
			else
			{
				UpdateDisplayText(null);
			}
			DirtyText();
		}

		private void OnControlDisposed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Control = null;
		}

		internal string FormatDateTime(DateTime date)
		{
			if (DateTimeFormat != null && DateTimeFormat.Length > 0)
			{
				try
				{
					return date.ToString(DateTimeFormat, FormatInfo);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			if (style == PanelStyle.Date)
			{
				return date.ToString("d", FormatInfo);
			}
			return date.ToString("t", FormatInfo);
		}

		private string FormatCharacterPosition(int character, int line, int characterCount, int lineCount)
		{
			try
			{
				if (characterPositionFormat != null && characterPositionFormat.Length > 0)
				{
					return GetFormattedCharacterString(characterPositionFormat, line, character, characterCount, lineCount);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return GetFormattedCharacterString(CharacterPositionFormatDefault, line, character, characterCount, lineCount);
		}

		private string GetFormattedCharacterString(string format, int line, int character, int characterCount, int lineCount)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(format);
			stringBuilder.Replace("[Line]", line.ToString(FormatInfo));
			stringBuilder.Replace("[Char]", character.ToString(FormatInfo));
			stringBuilder.Replace("[LineCount]", lineCount.ToString(FormatInfo));
			stringBuilder.Replace("[CharCount]", characterCount.ToString(FormatInfo));
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private string FormatCursorPosition(int x, int y)
		{
			try
			{
				if (cursorPositionFormat != null && cursorPositionFormat.Length > 0)
				{
					return GetFormattedCursorString(cursorPositionFormat, x, y);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return GetFormattedCursorString("[X],[Y]", x, y);
		}

		private string GetFormattedCursorString(string format, int x, int y)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(format);
			stringBuilder.Replace("[X]", x.ToString(FormatInfo));
			stringBuilder.Replace("[Y]", y.ToString(FormatInfo));
			if (Control != null)
			{
				stringBuilder.Replace("[Width]", Control.Width.ToString(FormatInfo));
				stringBuilder.Replace("[Height]", Control.Height.ToString(FormatInfo));
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private Size MeasurePanelText(Graphics g, Font f, StringFormat sf, string text)
		{
			//IL_01a0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01d2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01fb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_021b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0066: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f3: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00f9: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_010c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0112: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0164: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0144: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0115: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0121: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0126: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02b9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_03a1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (g == null || f == null)
			{
				return Size.Empty;
			}
			Size result = Size.Empty;
			switch (Style)
			{
			case PanelStyle.Marquee:
			{
				AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
				AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)192;
				ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				string text6 = Text;
				Direction marqueeDirection = MarqueeInfo.MarqueeDirection;
				MarqueeStyle marqueeStyle = MarqueeInfo.MarqueeStyle;
				if (marqueeDirection == Direction.TopToBottom || marqueeDirection == Direction.BottomToTop || marqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Flashing || marqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Typing)
				{
					sf.FormatFlags = StringFormatFlags.NoClip;
					UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)UIElement;
					int num = ((uIElement == null) ? Width : uIElement.Rect.Width);
					num -= Padding.Width * 2;
					if ((int)((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageHAlign != 2)
					{
						num -= autoSizeImage.Width;
					}
					if ((int)BorderStyleResolved != 1)
					{
						UIElementBorderWidths val = DrawUtility.CalculateBorderWidths(BorderStyleResolved, Border3DSide.All, uIElement);
						num -= ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val)).Left + ((UIElementBorderWidths)(ref val)).Top;
					}
					result = Size.Ceiling(DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, text6, f, num, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags));
				}
				else
				{
					result = Size.Ceiling(DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, text6, f, PointF.Empty, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags));
				}
				break;
			}
			case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
			{
				string text4 = FormatCharacterPosition(9999, 999999, 9999999, 999999);
				result = Size.Ceiling(DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, text4, f, PointF.Empty, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags));
				break;
			}
			case PanelStyle.CursorPosition:
			{
				string text3 = FormatCursorPosition(9999, 9999);
				result = Size.Ceiling(DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, text3, f, PointF.Empty, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags));
				break;
			}
			case PanelStyle.KeyState:
			{
				SizeF sizeF3 = DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, KeyStateInfo.OnTextResolved, f, PointF.Empty, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags);
				SizeF sizeF4 = DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, KeyStateInfo.OffTextResolved, f, PointF.Empty, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags);
				SizeF value = new SizeF(Math.Max(sizeF3.Width, sizeF4.Width), Math.Max(sizeF3.Height, sizeF4.Height));
				result = Size.Ceiling(value);
				break;
			}
			case PanelStyle.AutoStatusText:
			{
				UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
				if (ultraStatusBar == null)
				{
					return Size.Empty;
				}
				ICollection statusBarTexts = ultraStatusBar.GetStatusBarTexts();
				if (statusBarTexts == null)
				{
					return Size.Empty;
				}
				SizeF empty2 = SizeF.Empty;
				foreach (object item in statusBarTexts)
				{
					if (item is string text5)
					{
						SizeF sizeF2 = DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, text5, f, PointF.Empty, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags);
						if (sizeF2.Width > empty2.Width)
						{
							empty2.Width = sizeF2.Width;
						}
						if (sizeF2.Height > empty2.Height)
						{
							empty2.Height = sizeF2.Height;
						}
					}
				}
				result = Size.Ceiling(empty2);
				break;
			}
			case PanelStyle.Date:
			case PanelStyle.Time:
			{
				SizeF empty = SizeF.Empty;
				int year = DateTime.Today.Year;
				for (int i = 3; i <= 12; i++)
				{
					for (int j = 1; j <= 8; j++)
					{
						int[] array = new int[3] { 0, 12, 23 };
						for (int k = 0; k < 3; k++)
						{
							int hour = array[k];
							DateTime date = new DateTime(year, i, 21 + j, hour, 59, 59);
							string text2 = FormatDateTime(date);
							SizeF sizeF = DrawUtility.MeasureString(g, text2, f, PointF.Empty, sf, GdiDrawStringFlags);
							if (sizeF.Width > empty.Width)
							{
								empty.Width = sizeF.Width;
							}
							if (sizeF.Height > empty.Height)
							{
								empty.Height = sizeF.Height;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				result = Size.Ceiling(empty);
				break;
			}
			}
			if (result.Width < minWidth)
			{
				return new Size(minWidth, result.Height);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private Form GetForm()
		{
			if (UltraStatusBar == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Form result = null;
			try
			{
				((Control)(object)UltraStatusBar).FindForm();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void ResolveAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			if (UltraStatusBar == null)
			{
				if (HasAppearance)
				{
					Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				UltraStatusBar.ResolvePanelAppearance(this, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			//IL_014b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelType"), Style);
			if (Key != null && Key.Length > 0)
			{
				stringBuilder.AppendFormat(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelKey"), Key);
			}
			switch (Style)
			{
			case PanelStyle.ControlContainer:
				if (Control != null)
				{
					if (Control.Site != null)
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendFormat(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelControlContainer"), Control.Site.Name);
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder.AppendFormat(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelControlContainer"), Control.Name);
					}
				}
				break;
			case PanelStyle.KeyState:
				stringBuilder.AppendFormat(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelKeyState"), KeyStateInfo.Key);
				break;
			case PanelStyle.Marquee:
				stringBuilder.AppendFormat(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelMarquee"), MarqueeInfo.MarqueeStyle);
				break;
			case PanelStyle.Progress:
				stringBuilder.AppendFormat(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelProgress"), ProgressBarInfo.Style);
				break;
			case PanelStyle.StateButton:
				if (Checked)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LD_PanelChecked"));
				}
				break;
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public bool ShowCaretPositionDialog()
		{
			RichTextBox richTextBox = Control as RichTextBox;
			if (Style != PanelStyle.CharacterPosition || richTextBox == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			if (ultraStatusBar == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			CancelablePanelEventArgs e = new CancelablePanelEventArgs(this);
			ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.BeforeDisplayCharacterPositionDialog, e);
			if (e.Cancel)
			{
				return false;
			}
			CaretPositionDialog caretPositionDialog = new CaretPositionDialog();
			caretPositionDialog.InitializeDialog(richTextBox);
			Form form = null;
			try
			{
				form = GetForm();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (form == null)
				{
					caretPositionDialog.ShowDialog();
				}
				else
				{
					caretPositionDialog.ShowDialog(form);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.AfterDisplayCharacterPositionDialog, new PanelEventArgs(this));
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual AccessibleObject CreateAccessibilityInstance()
		{
			if (UltraStatusBar != null)
			{
				return UltraStatusBar.CreateAccessibilityInstance(this);
			}
			return ((AccessibleObjectWrapper)new UltraStatusPanelAccessibleObject(this)).AccessibleObject;
		}

				return appearanceHolder.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return false;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void ResetAppearance()
		{
			if (HasAppearance)
			{
				appearanceHolder.Reset();
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Appearance);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateAutoSizeInfo()
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0011: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00bd: Expected O, but got Unknown
			AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
			AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)137438953471L;
			ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			if (Style == PanelStyle.Progress)
			{
				autoSizeImage = Size.Empty;
			}
			else if (autoSizeImage == Size.Empty)
			{
				autoSizeImage = ImageAndTextUIElement.CalculateImageSize(((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).GetImage(UltraStatusBar.ImageList), UltraStatusBar.ScaleImages, UltraStatusBar.ScaledImageSize, false);
			}
			if (autoSizeText == Size.Empty)
			{
				MeasureStringDelegate val = null;
				switch (Style)
				{
				case PanelStyle.AutoStatusText:
				case PanelStyle.Date:
				case PanelStyle.KeyState:
				case PanelStyle.Marquee:
				case PanelStyle.Time:
					val = new MeasureStringDelegate(MeasurePanelText);
					break;
				}
				autoSizeText = ImageAndTextUIElement.CalculateTextSize(DisplayText, HotKeyPrefix, false, (Control)(object)UltraStatusBar, ref appearance, val);
			}
		}
