		protected virtual Rectangle GetNotificationBadgeBounds()
		{
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			PanelUIElement uIElement = UIElement;
			if (uIElement == null)
			{
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			Rectangle result = ((UIElement)uIElement).Rect;
			((UIElement)uIElement).VerifyChildElements();
			DisplayArea preferredDisplayArea = ((INotificationBadgeProvider)this).NotificationBadgeSettingsResolved.PreferredDisplayArea;
			switch ((int)preferredDisplayArea)
			{
			case 0:
			case 1:
			{
				Rectangle imageArea = GetImageArea();
				if (imageArea != Rectangle.Empty)
				{
					result = imageArea;
					break;
				}
				Rectangle textArea2 = GetTextArea();
				if (textArea2 != Rectangle.Empty)
				{
					result = textArea2;
				}
				break;
			}
			case 2:
			{
				Rectangle textArea = GetTextArea();
				if (textArea != Rectangle.Empty)
				{
					result = textArea;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			result.Inflate(-1, -1);
			return result;
		}

		private Rectangle GetImageArea()
		{
			PanelUIElement uIElement = UIElement;
			if (uIElement == null)
			{
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			UIElement descendant = ((UIElement)uIElement).GetDescendant(typeof(ImageUIElement));
			if (descendant != null)
			{
				return descendant.Rect;
			}
			return Rectangle.Empty;
		}

		private Rectangle GetTextArea()
		{
			PanelUIElement uIElement = UIElement;
			if (uIElement == null)
			{
				return Rectangle.Empty;
			}
			UIElement descendant = ((UIElement)uIElement).GetDescendant(typeof(TextUIElement));
			if (descendant != null)
			{
				return descendant.Rect;
			}
			return Rectangle.Empty;
		}

			}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraStatusBar_P_PanelsVisible")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(true)]
		public bool PanelsVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return panelsVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (panelsVisible != value)
				{
					bool panelsVisibleResolved = PanelsVisibleResolved;
					panelsVisible = value;
					if (PanelsVisible != panelsVisibleResolved)
					{
						((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
					}
					ManageKeyInfo();
					ManageTimer();
					AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents.Reorder);
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.PanelsVisible);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool PanelsVisibleResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (!panelsVisible)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (panels == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in panels)
				{
					if (panel.Visible)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

				return padding;

				if ((padding == null || !padding.Equals(value)) && (padding != null || value != null))

					{
						padding.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					}
					padding = value;
					if (padding != null)
					{
						padding.SubObjectPropChanged += SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					}
					((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.Padding);
				}
			}
		}

		{

			}
		}

		}

		internal ICollection GetStatusBarTexts()
		{
			if (statusBarTexts == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return statusBarTexts.Values;
		}

		private void AttachUserPreferenceListener()
		{
			SystemEvents.UserPreferenceChanged += UserPreferenceChangedHandler;
		}

		private void DetachUserPreferenceListener()
		{
			SystemEvents.UserPreferenceChanged -= UserPreferenceChangedHandler;
		}

		private void OnUserPreferenceChanged(object sender, UserPreferenceChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((Control)this).InvokeRequired)
			{
				try
				{
					((Control)this).Invoke((Delegate)new UserPreferenceChangedEventHandler(OnUserPreferenceChanged), new object[2] { sender, e });
					return;
				}
				catch
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			DirtyPanels(dirtyText: true, dirtyImage: false);
		}

		private void UpdateStatusBarText()
		{
			string text = ((!autoSense) ? externalStatusText : internalStatusText);
			if (text == lastStatusText)
			{
				return;
			}
			lastStatusText = text;
			if (((Control)this).Disposing || ((Control)this).IsDisposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in Panels)
			{
				if (panel.Style == PanelStyle.AutoStatusText)
				{
					panel.UpdateDisplayText(text);
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnExtenderMenuSelect(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (panels != null)
			{
				internalStatusText = GetStatusBarText(sender as Component);
				UpdateStatusBarText();
			}
		}

		private void OnExtenderMouseEnter(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (panels != null)
			{
				internalStatusText = GetStatusBarText(sender as Component);
				currentExtenderItem = sender;
				UpdateStatusBarText();
			}
		}

		private void OnExtenderMouseLeave(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (panels != null)
			{
				internalStatusText = string.Empty;
				currentExtenderItem = null;
				UpdateStatusBarText();
			}
		}

		private void OnExtenderItemDisposed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			SetStatusBarText(sender as Component, "");
		}

		private void HookExtenderComponent(Component component)
		{
			if (component is MenuItem)
			{
				((MenuItem)component).Select += ExtenderMenuSelectHandler;
				((MenuItem)component).Disposed += ExtenderItemDisposedHandler;
			}
			else if (component is Control)
			{
				Control control = (Control)component;
				control.MouseEnter += ExtenderMouseEnterHandler;
				control.MouseLeave += ExtenderMouseLeaveHandler;
				control.Disposed += ExtenderItemDisposedHandler;
			}
		}

		private void UnhookExtenderComponent(Component component)
		{
			if (component is MenuItem)
			{
				((MenuItem)component).Select -= ExtenderMenuSelectHandler;
				((MenuItem)component).Disposed -= ExtenderItemDisposedHandler;
			}
			else if (component is Control)
			{
				Control control = (Control)component;
				control.MouseEnter -= ExtenderMouseEnterHandler;
				control.MouseLeave -= ExtenderMouseLeaveHandler;
				control.Disposed -= ExtenderItemDisposedHandler;
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_UltraStatusBar_M_GetStatusBarText")]
		[DefaultValue(null)]
		public string GetStatusBarText(Component component)
		{
			if (!StatusBarTexts.Contains(component))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return (string)StatusBarTexts[component];
		}

		public void SetStatusBarText(Component component, string text)
		{
			if (component == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			bool flag = StatusBarTexts.Contains(component);
			bool flag2 = text == null || text.Length == 0;
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				UnhookExtenderComponent(component);
				StatusBarTexts.Remove(component);
			}
			else if (!flag && !flag2)
			{
				StatusBarTexts.Add(component, text);
				HookExtenderComponent(component);
			}
			else if (flag && !flag2)
			{
				StatusBarTexts[component] = text;
			}
			if (component == currentExtenderItem)
			{
				internalStatusText = text;
				if (flag2)
				{
					currentExtenderItem = null;
				}
				UpdateStatusBarText();
			}
			else if (!flag2 && component is Control && ((Control)component).IsHandleCreated)
			{
				IntPtr windowHandle = NativeWindowMethods.GetWindowHandle(Control.MousePosition);
				IntPtr handle = ((Control)component).Handle;
				if (handle == windowHandle)
				{
					internalStatusText = text;
					UpdateStatusBarText();
					currentExtenderItem = component;
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnTimeElapsed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (((Control)this).Disposing || Panels.Disposed || ((Control)this).Capture)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in Panels)
			{
				if (!panel.Disposed && panel.Visible && (panel.Style == PanelStyle.Date || panel.Style == PanelStyle.Time))
				{
					panel.UpdateDisplayText(panel.FormatDateTime(DateTime.Now));
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnKeyStateChanged(object sender, KeyStateChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (((Control)this).Disposing || Panels.Disposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in Panels)
			{
				if (!panel.Disposed && panel.Visible && panel.Style == PanelStyle.KeyState && panel.KeyStateInfo.Key == (KeyState)e.KeyCode)
				{
					panel.KeyStateInfo.Toggled = e.IsKeyDown;
					panel.UpdateDisplayText(panel.DisplayText);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ManageKeyInfo()
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (!IsDisposing && PanelsVisibleResolved)
			{
				foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in Panels)
				{
					if (panel.Visible && !panel.Disposed)
					{
						PanelStyle style = panel.Style;
						if (style == PanelStyle.KeyState)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag && !keyInfoIsAttached)
			{
				keyInfoIsAttached = true;
				KeyStateManager.KeyStateChanged += KeyStateChangedHandler;
			}
			else if (!flag && keyInfoIsAttached)
			{
				keyInfoIsAttached = false;
				KeyStateManager.KeyStateChanged -= KeyStateChangedHandler;
			}
		}

		private void ManageTimer()
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (!IsDisposing && PanelsVisibleResolved)
			{
				foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in Panels)
				{
					if (panel.Visible && !panel.Disposed)
					{
						PanelStyle style = panel.Style;
						if (style == PanelStyle.Date || style == PanelStyle.Time)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag && !timerIsAttached)
			{
				TimerManager.TimeElapsed += TimeElapsedHandler;
				timerIsAttached = true;
			}
			else if (!flag && timerIsAttached)
			{
				TimerManager.TimeElapsed -= TimeElapsedHandler;
				timerIsAttached = false;
			}
		}

		private void OnSubObjectPropChange(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (propChangeInfo.Source == appearances)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
				((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.Appearances, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == appearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				DirtyPanels(dirtyText: true, dirtyImage: false);
				((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.Appearance, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasPanelAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == panelAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				DirtyPanels(dirtyText: true, dirtyImage: true);
				((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.PanelAppearance, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (panels != null && propChangeInfo.Source == panels)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
				ManageTimer();
				ManageKeyInfo();
				if (!IsDesignMode || propChangeInfo.FindPropId(StatusBarPropertyIds.KeyStateToggled) == null)
				{
					((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.Panels, propChangeInfo);
				}
			}
			else if (padding == propChangeInfo.Source)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
				((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.Padding);
			}
			else if (HasNotificationBadgeSettings && propChangeInfo.Source == notificationBadgeSettings)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
				((UltraControlBase)this).NotifyPropChange((Enum)StatusBarPropertyIds.NotificationBadgeSettings, propChangeInfo);
			}
		}

		public void ResolvePanelAppearance(UltraStatusPanel panel, ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			{
				val.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (RoleState)1);
			}
			if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseControlInfo)
			{
				if (panel != null && panel.HasAppearance)
				{
					panel.Appearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
				if (HasPanelAppearance)
				{
					PanelAppearance.MergeData(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
				}
			}

				val.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (RoleState)1);

			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).TextTrimming = (TextTrimming)3;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps & 0xFFFFFFFFDFFFFFFFuL);
			}

					break;

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				isDisposing = true;
				((DisposableObject)(object)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).Dispose();
				controlElement = null;
				if (HasAppearance)
				{
					appearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					appearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (HasPanelAppearance)
				{
					panelAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					panelAppearanceHolder = null;
				}
				if (toolTip != null)
				{
					toolTip.Dispose();
					toolTip = null;
				}
				if (toolTipEx != null)
				{
					toolTipEx.Hide();
					((DisposableObject)(object)toolTipEx).Dispose();
					toolTipEx = null;
				}
				if (notificationBadgeSettings != null)
				{
					((SubObjectBase)(object)notificationBadgeSettings).SubObjectPropChanged -= SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
					((DisposableObject)(object)notificationBadgeSettings).Dispose();
					notificationBadgeSettings = null;
				}
				ManageTimer();
				ManageKeyInfo();
				try
				{
					DetachUserPreferenceListener();
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				if (statusBarTexts != null)
				{
					ICollection keys = StatusBarTexts.Keys;
					foreach (object item in keys)
					{
						UnhookExtenderComponent(item as Component);
					}
					statusBarTexts.Clear();
					statusBarTexts = null;
					currentExtenderItem = null;
				}
				if (panels != null)
				{
					foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in panels)
					{
						if (!panel.Disposed)
						{
							panel.Dispose();
						}
					}
					if (!panels.Disposed)
					{
						panels.Dispose();
					}
				}
				((IStatusBar)this).ToolbarsManager = null;
				UnhookForm();
			}
			((UltraControlBase)this).Dispose(disposing);
		}

		protected override bool IsPointDesignTimeActive(Point pt)
		{
			UIElement val = ((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).ElementFromPoint(pt, false);
			if (val != null && val.PointInAdjustableArea(pt))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return ((UltraControlBase)this).IsPointDesignTimeActive(pt);
		}

		protected internal void DisplayPanelToolTip(UltraStatusPanel panel)
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode && panel.ToolTipText != null && panel.ToolTipText.Length != 0 && showToolTips)
			{
				ToolTipEx.ToolTipText = panel.ToolTipText;
				ToolTipEx.Show(false);
			}
		}

		protected internal void HidePanelToolTip()
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode)
			{
				if (toolTip != null)
				{
					toolTip.RemoveAll();
					toolTip.Dispose();
					toolTip = null;
				}
				if (toolTipEx != null)
				{
					ToolTipEx.Hide();
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal void DisplayMDIPanelToolTip(UltraStatusPanel panel, string text)
		{
			if (!((Component)this).DesignMode && text != null && text.Length != 0 && showToolTips)
			{
				ToolTipEx.ToolTipText = text;
				ToolTipEx.Show(false);
			}
		}

		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if ((int)SizeGripVisible == 0)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
			}
			((Control)this).OnDockChanged(e);
		}

		protected override void OnLocationChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if ((int)SizeGripVisible == 0)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
			}
			((Control)this).OnLocationChanged(e);
		}

		private void OnMDIChildActivate(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (panels == null || panels.Disposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in panels)
			{
				if (panel.Style == PanelStyle.MDIList && panel.Visible)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (flag && !((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).IsDrawing)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements();
			}
		}

		private void HookForm()
		{
			UnhookForm();
			if (((Control)this).Disposing)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				GetParentForm();
				if (parentForm != null)
				{
					parentForm.MdiChildActivate += MDIChildActivateHandler;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private void GetParentForm()
		{
			parentForm = ((Control)this).FindForm();
			if (parentForm != null)
			{
				lastWindowState = parentForm.WindowState;
			}
		}

		private void UnhookForm()
		{
			if (parentForm != null)
			{
				try
				{
					parentForm.MdiChildActivate -= MDIChildActivateHandler;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
				parentForm = null;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnParentChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnParentChanged(e);
			HookForm();
		}

		internal Control GetContainer()
		{
			try
			{
				UIPermission uIPermission = new UIPermission(UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					uIPermission.Assert();
				}
				return ((Control)this).Parent;
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		private bool IsResizableFormContainer()
		{
			if (CanResizeContainer())
			{
				Control container = GetContainer();
				return container is Form;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal bool CanResizeContainer()
		{
			//IL_0073: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0078: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007d: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_007f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0082: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			Control container = GetContainer();
			if (container == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Rectangle clientRectangle = container.ClientRectangle;
			Rectangle bounds = ((Control)this).Bounds;
			if ((toolbarsManager == null || !(container is Form)) && (bounds.Bottom != clientRectangle.Height || bounds.Right != clientRectangle.Width))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (container is Form)
			{
				Form form = (Form)container;
				if (form.WindowState != FormWindowState.Normal)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (toolbarsManager != null)
				{
					FormDisplayStyle val = toolbarsManager.FormDisplayStyle;
					if ((int)val == 1)
					{
						return true;
					}
					if ((int)val == 2)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				FormBorderStyle formBorderStyle = form.FormBorderStyle;
				if (formBorderStyle != FormBorderStyle.Sizable && formBorderStyle != FormBorderStyle.SizableToolWindow)
				{
					if (form.IsHandleCreated)
					{
						try
						{
							int windowLongApi = NativeWindowMethods.GetWindowLongApi(form.Handle, -16);
							return (windowLongApi & 0x40000) != 0;
						}
						catch (SecurityException)
						{
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal void ReparentControl(Control control, Control newParent)
		{
			if (control == null || IsDisposing)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				Control parent = control.Parent;
				if (parent == newParent)
				{
					return;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				if (parent != null)
				{
					Form form = parent.FindForm();
					if (form != null)
					{
						flag = Utilities.IsFormActive(form);
						if (form.ActiveControl == control || control.Contains(form.ActiveControl))
						{
							form.ActiveControl = control.Parent;
						}
					}
					parent.Controls.Remove(control);
				}
				if (newParent != null)
				{
					newParent.SuspendLayout();
					Form form2 = newParent.FindForm();
					form2?.SuspendLayout();
					control.Dock = DockStyle.None;
					newParent.Controls.AddRange(new Control[1] { control });
					control.Parent = newParent;
					if (form2 != null)
					{
						newParent.ResumeLayout(performLayout: false);
						form2.ResumeLayout(performLayout: false);
						if (flag)
						{
							form2.Activate();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						newParent.ResumeLayout(performLayout: false);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					control.Parent = ((Control)this).Parent;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		protected override void OnCreateControl()
		{
			HookForm();
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnCreateControl();
		}

		protected override void OnOffice2007ColorSchemeChanged()
		{
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnOffice2007ColorSchemeChanged();
			if (((Control)this).IsHandleCreated && !((Control)this).IsDisposed && ViewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.Office2007)
			{
				((UltraControlBase)this).Invalidate();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnResize(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (toolbarsManager != null && ParentForm != null && IsFormRounded && IsDockedBottom)
			{
				FormWindowState windowState = ParentForm.WindowState;
				if (windowState != FormWindowState.Minimized)
				{
					bool flag = lastWindowState.HasValue && lastWindowState.Value != windowState;
					lastWindowState = windowState;
					if (flag)
					{
						int num = XPThemes.FrameBorderSize.Width - ToolbarsManager.RoundedFormBorderWidth;
						switch (windowState)
						{
						case FormWindowState.Maximized:
							((Control)this).SetBounds(((Control)this).Left, ((Control)this).Top - num, ((Control)this).Width, ((Control)this).Height + num);
							break;
						case FormWindowState.Normal:
							((Control)this).SetBounds(((Control)this).Left, ((Control)this).Top + num, ((Control)this).Width, ((Control)this).Height - num);
							break;
						}
						if (num != 0)
						{
							return;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			StyleUtils.DirtyCachedPropertyVals(this);
			DirtyPanels(dirtyText: false, dirtyImage: false, dirtyCachedPropertyValues: true);
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnResize(e);
		}

		internal ColorMap[] GetTransparentColorMap(UltraStatusPanel panel)
		{
			//IL_0002: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0030: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I8
			AppearanceData appearance = default(AppearanceData);
			AppearancePropFlags requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)256;
			if (panel == null)
			{
				ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			else
			{
				ResolvePanelAppearance(panel, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			if ((long)requestedProps == 256 || !((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).HasPropertyBeenSet((AppearancePropFlags)256))
			{
				return null;
			}
			object image = ((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).Image;
			if (image == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Color transparentColor = ImageTransparentColor;
			if (ImageList != null && ImageList.TransparentColor != Color.Transparent && ImageList.TransparentColor != Color.Empty && !(image is Image))
			{
				transparentColor = ImageList.TransparentColor;
			}
			if (transparentColor == Color.Transparent || transparentColor == Color.Empty)
			{
				return null;
			}
			ColorMap colorMap = new ColorMap();
			colorMap.NewColor = Color.Transparent;
			colorMap.OldColor = transparentColor;
			return new ColorMap[1] { colorMap };
		}

		[UIPermission(SecurityAction.InheritanceDemand, Window = UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows)]
		[UIPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Window = UIPermissionWindow.AllWindows)]
		protected override bool ProcessMnemonic(char charCode)
		{
			if (((Control)this).Visible && ((Control)this).Enabled && PanelsVisibleResolved)
			{
				UltraStatusPanel[] array = (UltraStatusPanel[])Panels.All;
				bool flag = false;
				UltraStatusPanel[] array2 = array;
				foreach (UltraStatusPanel ultraStatusPanel in array2)
				{
					if (!ultraStatusPanel.Visible || !ultraStatusPanel.Enabled)
					{
						continue;
					}
					PanelStyle style = ultraStatusPanel.Style;
					if ((style == PanelStyle.Button || style == PanelStyle.StateButton) && Control.IsMnemonic(charCode, ultraStatusPanel.Text))
					{
						PanelEventArgs e = new PanelEventArgs(ultraStatusPanel);
						if (ultraStatusPanel.Style == PanelStyle.StateButton)
						{
							ultraStatusPanel.Checked = !ultraStatusPanel.Checked;
						}
						if (ultraStatusPanel.Style == PanelStyle.Button)
						{
							FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.ButtonClick, e);
						}
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return ((Control)this).ProcessMnemonic(charCode);
		}

		protected override AccessibleObject CreateAccessibilityInstance()
		{
			return CreateAccessibilityInstance(this);
		}

		protected internal virtual AccessibleObject CreateAccessibilityInstance(object relatedObject)
		{
			if (relatedObject is SizeGripUIElement)
			{
				return ((AccessibleObjectWrapper)new SizeGripUIElement.SizeGripAccessibleObject(relatedObject as SizeGripUIElement)).AccessibleObject;
			}
			if (relatedObject is UltraStatusPanel)
			{
				return ((AccessibleObjectWrapper)new UltraStatusPanel.UltraStatusPanelAccessibleObject(relatedObject as UltraStatusPanel)).AccessibleObject;
			}
			if (relatedObject is MDIButtonUIElement)
			{
				return ((AccessibleObjectWrapper)new MDIButtonUIElement.MdiButtonAccessibleObject(relatedObject as MDIButtonUIElement)).AccessibleObject;
			}
			if (relatedObject == this)
			{
				return (AccessibleObject)(object)new UltraStatusBarAccessibleObject(this);
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal void AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents accEvent, int childID)
		{
			if (((Control)this).IsHandleCreated && !((Control)this).Disposing)
			{
				((Control)this).AccessibilityNotifyClients(accEvent, childID);
			}
		}

		internal void AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(AccessibleEvents accEvent)
		{
			AccessibilityNotifyClientsInternal(accEvent, -1);
		}

		private void DirtyPanels(bool dirtyText, bool dirtyImage)
		{
			DirtyPanels(dirtyText, dirtyImage, dirtyCachedPropertyValues: false);
		}

		private void DirtyPanels(bool dirtyText, bool dirtyImage, bool dirtyCachedPropertyValues)
		{
			if (((Control)this).Disposing || ((Control)this).IsDisposed)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in Panels)
			{
				if (!panel.Disposed)
				{
					if (dirtyImage)
					{
						panel.DirtyImage();
					}
					if (dirtyText)
					{
						panel.DirtyText();
					}
					if (dirtyCachedPropertyValues)
					{
						panel.DirtyCachedPropertyValues();
					}
				}
			}
			if (controlElement != null)
			{
				((UIElement)((UltraControlBase)this).ControlUIElement).DirtyChildElements(true);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnFontChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnFontChanged(e);
			DirtyPanels(dirtyText: true, dirtyImage: false);
		}

		protected override ComponentRole CreateComponentRole()

		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ChangeInfo.PropId is StatusBarPropertyIds)
			{
				StatusBarPropertyIds statusBarPropertyIds = (StatusBarPropertyIds)(object)e.ChangeInfo.PropId;
				switch (statusBarPropertyIds)
				{
				case StatusBarPropertyIds.BorderStyle:
				case StatusBarPropertyIds.BorderStylePanel:
				case StatusBarPropertyIds.SizeGripVisible:
				case StatusBarPropertyIds.ButtonStyle:
				case StatusBarPropertyIds.ViewStyle:
					StyleUtils.DirtyCachedPropertyVals(this);
					DirtyPanels(dirtyText: false, dirtyImage: false, dirtyCachedPropertyValues: true);
					break;
				}
				if (statusBarPropertyIds == StatusBarPropertyIds.ViewStyle)
				{
					DirtyPanels(dirtyText: true, dirtyImage: false);
				}
			}
			((UltraControlBase)this).OnPropertyChanged(e);
		}

		internal void FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds id, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!EventManager.CanFireEvent(id) || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(StatusBarEventIds), id))
			{
				return;
			}
			EventManager.IncrementInProgress(id);
			try
			{
				switch (id)
				{
				case StatusBarEventIds.AfterAutoSizePanel:
					OnAfterAutoSizePanel(e as PanelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.AfterDisplayCharacterPositionDialog:
					OnAfterDisplayCharacterPositionDialog(e as PanelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.AfterDragResize:
					OnAfterDragResize(e as PanelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.PanelClick:
					OnPanelClick(e as PanelClickEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.BeforeAutoSizePanel:
					OnBeforeAutoSizePanel(e as CancelablePanelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.BeforeDisplayCharacterPositionDialog:
					OnBeforeDisplayCharacterPositionDialog(e as CancelablePanelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.BeforeDragResize:
					OnBeforeDragResize(e as CancelablePanelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.BeforeDragSizeGrip:
					OnBeforeDragSizeGrip(e as CancelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.ButtonClick:
					OnButtonClick(e as PanelEventArgs);
					break;
				case StatusBarEventIds.PanelDoubleClick:
					OnPanelDoubleClick(e as PanelClickEventArgs);
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
			finally
			{
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(id);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnterElement(UIElementEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!EventManager.CanFireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.MouseEnterElement))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_26"));
			}
			EventManager.IncrementInProgress(StatusBarEventIds.MouseEnterElement);
			try
			{
				((UltraControlBase)this).OnMouseEnterElement(e);
			}
			catch
			{
				throw;
			}
			finally
			{
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(StatusBarEventIds.MouseEnterElement);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeaveElement(UIElementEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!EventManager.CanFireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.MouseLeaveElement))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_26"));
			}
			EventManager.IncrementInProgress(StatusBarEventIds.MouseLeaveElement);
			try
			{
				((UltraControlBase)this).OnMouseLeaveElement(e);
			}
			catch
			{
				throw;
			}
			finally
			{
				EventManager.DecrementInProgress(StatusBarEventIds.MouseLeaveElement);
			}
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.BeginInit()
		{
			initializing = true;
			if (panels == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (UltraStatusPanel panel in panels)
			{
				panel.Inherited = true;
			}
		}

		{
			initializing = false;
		}

		internal void ResolveOffice2007PanelButtonAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearanceData, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps, UltraStatusPanel panel, UIRole role, bool enabled, bool isMouseDown, bool isStateButton, bool isStateButtonDown, bool isMouseOver, GradientStyle activeGradient = (GradientStyle)26)
		{

	public class StatusBarEventManager : EventManagerBase
	{
		private UltraStatusBar control;

		private static int[] beforeEventIndexes;

		private static int[] afterEventIndexes;

		public UltraStatusBar StatusBar => control;

		protected int[] AfterEventIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				if (afterEventIndexes == null)
				{
					afterEventIndexes = new int[3] { 3, 5, 8 };
				}
				return afterEventIndexes;
			}
		}

		protected int[] BeforeEventIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				if (beforeEventIndexes == null)
				{
					beforeEventIndexes = new int[4] { 2, 4, 9, 10 };
				}
				return beforeEventIndexes;
			}
		}

		public StatusBarEventManager(UltraStatusBar control)
			: base(Enum.GetValues(typeof(StatusBarEventIds)).Length)
		{
			if (control == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_0"));
			}
			this.control = control;
		}

		public bool InProgress(StatusBarEventIds eventid)
		{
			return InProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		public bool IsEnabled(StatusBarEventIds eventid)
		{
			return IsEnabled((int)eventid);
		}

		public void SetEnabled(StatusBarEventIds eventid, bool enabled)
		{
			SetEnabled((int)eventid, enabled);
		}

		public bool IsEnabled(StatusBarEventGroups group)
		{
			return group switch
			{
				StatusBarEventGroups.AllEvents => base.AllEventsEnabled, 
				StatusBarEventGroups.BeforeEvents => IsEnabled(BeforeEventIndexes), 
				StatusBarEventGroups.AfterEvents => IsEnabled(AfterEventIndexes), 
				_ => false, 
			};
		}

		public void SetEnabled(StatusBarEventGroups group, bool enabled)
		{
			switch (group)
			{
			case StatusBarEventGroups.AllEvents:
				base.AllEventsEnabled = enabled;
				break;
			case StatusBarEventGroups.BeforeEvents:
				SetEnabled(BeforeEventIndexes, enabled);
				break;
			case StatusBarEventGroups.AfterEvents:
				SetEnabled(AfterEventIndexes, enabled);
				break;
			}
		}

		internal void IncrementInProgress(StatusBarEventIds eventid)
		{
			IncrementInProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		internal void DecrementInProgress(StatusBarEventIds eventid)
		{
			DecrementInProgress((int)eventid);
		}

		public bool CanFireEvent(StatusBarEventIds eventid)
		{
			return IsEnabled(eventid);
		}
	}
	public enum StatusBarEventIds
	{
		MouseEnterElement,
		MouseLeaveElement,
		BeforeAutoSizePanel,
		AfterAutoSizePanel,
		BeforeDisplayCharacterPositionDialog,
		AfterDisplayCharacterPositionDialog,
		PanelDoubleClick,
		ButtonClick,
		AfterDragResize,
		BeforeDragResize,
		BeforeDragSizeGrip,
		PanelClick
	}
	public enum StatusBarEventGroups
	{
		AllEvents,
		BeforeEvents,
		AfterEvents
	}

	[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
	public class KeyStateInfo : SubObjectBase
	{
		internal const string ScrollLockEnabled = "SCRL";

		internal const string CapsLockEnabled = "CAPS";

		internal const string InsertLockEnabled = "INS";

		internal const string NumLockEnabled = "NUM";

		internal const string InsertLockDisabled = "OVR";

		internal const string KanaEnabled = "KANA";

		private const KeyStateDisplayStyle KeyStateDisplayStyleDefault = KeyStateDisplayStyle.ChangeText;

		private KeyStateDisplayStyle displayStyle = KeyStateDisplayStyle.ChangeText;

		private string onText = string.Empty;

		private string offText = string.Empty;

		private const KeyState KeyDefault = KeyState.NumLock;

		private KeyState key = KeyState.NumLock;

		private bool toggled;

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_KeyStateInfo_P_DisplayStyle")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(KeyStateDisplayStyle.ChangeText)]
		public KeyStateDisplayStyle DisplayStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return displayStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (displayStyle != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(KeyStateDisplayStyle), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(KeyStateDisplayStyle));
					}
					displayStyle = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.DisplayStyle);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public bool Toggled
		{
			get
			{
				return toggled;
			}
			set
			{
				if (toggled != value)
				{
					toggled = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.KeyStateToggled);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_KeyStateInfo_P_Key")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		[DefaultValue(KeyState.NumLock)]
		public KeyState Key
		{
			get
			{
				return key;
			}
			set
			{
				if (key != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(KeyState), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(KeyState));
					}
					key = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Key);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public string OnTextResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (onText.Length > 0)
				{
					return onText;
				}
				return key switch
				{
					KeyState.InsertMode => "INS", 
					KeyState.CapsLock => "CAPS", 
					KeyState.ScrollLock => "SCRL", 
					KeyState.Kana => "KANA", 
					_ => "NUM", 
				};
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_KeyStateInfo_P_OnText")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue("")]
		[Localizable(true)]
		public string OnText
		{
			get
			{
				return onText;
			}
			set
			{
				value = ((value == null) ? string.Empty : value);
				if (!onText.Equals(value))
				{
					onText = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.OnText);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_KeyStateInfo_P_OffText")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue("")]
		[Localizable(true)]
		public string OffText
		{
			get
			{
				return offText;
			}
			set
			{
				value = ((value == null) ? string.Empty : value);
				if (!offText.Equals(value))
				{
					offText = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.OffText);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public string OffTextResolved
		{
			get
			{
				if (offText.Length > 0)
				{
					return offText;
				}
				return key switch
				{
					KeyState.InsertMode => "OVR", 
					_ => string.Empty, 
				};
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (DisplayStyle == KeyStateDisplayStyle.ChangeText && Key == KeyState.NumLock && "".Equals(OnText))
			{
				return !"".Equals(OffText);
			}
			return true;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void Reset()
		{
			DisplayStyle = KeyStateDisplayStyle.ChangeText;
			Key = KeyState.NumLock;
			OnText = string.Empty;
			OffText = string.Empty;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"KeyStateInfo [{Key}], Display Style = {DisplayStyle}";
		}
	}
	internal class KeyStateManager : DisposableObject, IMessageFilter
	{
		private static KeyStateManager keyManager;

		private static KeyStateChangedEventHandler keyStateChanged;

		private byte[] oldState;

		private byte[] newState;

		private EventHandler timeElapsedHandler;

		private const int WM_KEYDOWN = 256;

		private const int WM_ACTIVATEAPP = 28;

		private EventHandler TimeElapsedHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (timeElapsedHandler == null)
				{
					timeElapsedHandler = OnTimeElapsed;
				}
				return timeElapsedHandler;
			}
		}

		internal static event KeyStateChangedEventHandler KeyStateChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddEventHandler(value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveEventHandler(value);
			}
		}

		public static bool GetKeyState(KeyState vKey)
		{
			lock (typeof(KeyStateManager))
			{
				try
				{
					return InternalGetKeyState(vKey);
				}
				catch
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		private static bool InternalGetKeyState(KeyState key)
		{
			short keyState = NativeWindowMethods.GetKeyState((int)key);
			return (keyState & 1) == 1;
		}

		private bool IsKeyDown(Keys key)
		{
			return (newState[(int)key] & 1) != 0;
		}

		private KeyStateManager()
		{
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			oldState = new byte[256];
			oldState.Initialize();
			newState = new byte[256];
			newState.Initialize();
			try
			{
				GetKeyboardState();
				if (Form.ActiveForm == null)
				{
					TimerManager.TimeElapsed += TimeElapsedHandler;
				}
				Application.AddMessageFilter(this);
				DropDownManager.ActivationChanged += new ActivationChangedEventHandler(OnApplicationActivationChanged);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		~KeyStateManager()
		{
			Dispose();
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			//IL_0028: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0032: Expected O, but got Unknown
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
			if (timeElapsedHandler != null)
			{
				TimerManager.TimeElapsed -= timeElapsedHandler;
				timeElapsedHandler = null;
			}
			try
			{
				DropDownManager.ActivationChanged -= new ActivationChangedEventHandler(OnApplicationActivationChanged);
				Application.RemoveMessageFilter(this);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			base.Dispose();
		}

		private static void AddEventHandler(KeyStateChangedEventHandler handler)
		{
			lock (typeof(KeyStateManager))
			{
				if (keyStateChanged == null)
				{
					keyStateChanged = handler;
					keyManager = new KeyStateManager();
				}
				else
				{
					keyStateChanged = (KeyStateChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(keyStateChanged, handler);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void RemoveEventHandler(KeyStateChangedEventHandler handler)
		{
			lock (typeof(KeyStateManager))
			{
				if (keyStateChanged != null)
				{
					keyStateChanged = (KeyStateChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(keyStateChanged, handler);
				}
				if (keyStateChanged == null && keyManager != null)
				{
					keyManager.Dispose();
					keyManager = null;
				}
			}
		}

		bool IMessageFilter.PreFilterMessage(ref Message msg)
		{
			if (msg.Msg == 256)
			{
				int num = msg.WParam.ToInt32();
				switch ((Keys)(num & 0xFFFF))
				{
				case Keys.Capital:
				case Keys.KanaMode:
				case Keys.Insert:
				case Keys.NumLock:
				case Keys.Scroll:
					GetKeyboardState();
					break;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void OnApplicationActivationChanged(object sender, ActivationChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.HookCode == 28 && !base.Disposed)
			{
				if (e.HwndActivated == IntPtr.Zero)
				{
					TimerManager.TimeElapsed += TimeElapsedHandler;
				}
				else
				{
					TimerManager.TimeElapsed -= TimeElapsedHandler;
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnTimeElapsed(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
				if (Utilities.CanAssert)
				{
					securityPermission.Assert();
				}
				GetKeyboardState();
				CodeAccessPermission.RevertAssert();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private void GetKeyboardState()
		{
			lock (typeof(KeyStateManager))
			{
				newState.CopyTo(oldState, 0);
				if (Form.ActiveForm != null)
				{
					int keyboardState = NativeWindowMethods.GetKeyboardState(newState);
				}
				else
				{
					newState[20] = (byte)NativeWindowMethods.GetKeyState(20);
					newState[45] = (byte)NativeWindowMethods.GetKeyState(45);
					newState[21] = (byte)NativeWindowMethods.GetKeyState(21);
					newState[144] = (byte)NativeWindowMethods.GetKeyState(144);
					newState[145] = (byte)NativeWindowMethods.GetKeyState(145);
				}
				if (keyStateChanged != null)
				{
					if (newState[20] != oldState[20])
					{
						keyStateChanged(null, new KeyStateChangedEventArgs(Keys.Capital, IsKeyDown(Keys.Capital)));
					}
					if (newState[45] != oldState[45])
					{
						keyStateChanged(null, new KeyStateChangedEventArgs(Keys.Insert, IsKeyDown(Keys.Insert)));
					}
					if (newState[21] != oldState[21])
					{
						keyStateChanged(null, new KeyStateChangedEventArgs(Keys.KanaMode, IsKeyDown(Keys.KanaMode)));
					}
					if (newState[144] != oldState[144])
					{
						keyStateChanged(null, new KeyStateChangedEventArgs(Keys.NumLock, IsKeyDown(Keys.NumLock)));
					}
					if (newState[145] != oldState[145])
					{
						keyStateChanged(null, new KeyStateChangedEventArgs(Keys.Scroll, IsKeyDown(Keys.Scroll)));
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal class KeyStateChangedEventArgs : KeyEventArgs
	{
		private bool isKeyDown;

		public bool IsKeyDown => isKeyDown;

		public KeyStateChangedEventArgs(Keys key, bool down)
			: base(key)
		{
			isKeyDown = down;
		}
	}
	internal delegate void KeyStateChangedEventHandler(object sender, KeyStateChangedEventArgs e);
	[TypeConverter(typeof(UIElementMarginsTypeConverter))]
	public class UIElementMargins : SubObjectBase, ICloneable
	{
		public sealed class UIElementMarginsTypeConverter : ExpandableObjectConverter
		{
			public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
			{
				if (destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
			}

			public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
			{
				if (destinationType == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_10"));
				}
				if (destinationType == typeof(InstanceDescriptor) && value is UIElementMargins)
				{
					UIElementMargins uIElementMargins = (UIElementMargins)value;
					ConstructorInfo constructor = typeof(UIElementMargins).GetConstructor(new Type[4]
					{
						typeof(int),
						typeof(int),
						typeof(int),
						typeof(int)
					});
					if (constructor != null)
					{
						return new InstanceDescriptor(constructor, new object[4] { uIElementMargins.left, uIElementMargins.top, uIElementMargins.right, uIElementMargins.bottom }, isComplete: true);
					}
				}
				return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
			}
		}

		private int left = -1;

		private int top = -1;

		private int right = -1;

		private int bottom = -1;

		[NotifyParentProperty(true)]
		[DefaultValue(-1)]
		public int Left
		{
			get
			{
				return left;
			}
			set
			{
				if (left != value)
				{
					if (value < -1)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_1"), value, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_2"));
					}
					left = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Left);
				}
			}
		}

		[NotifyParentProperty(true)]
		[DefaultValue(-1)]
		public int Top
		{
			get
			{
				return top;
			}
			set
			{
				if (top != value)
				{
					if (value < -1)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_3"), value, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_4"));
					}
					top = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Top);
				}
			}
		}

		[NotifyParentProperty(true)]
		[DefaultValue(-1)]
		public int Right
		{
			get
			{
				return right;
			}
			set
			{
				if (right != value)
				{
					if (value < -1)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_5"), value, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_6"));
					}
					right = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Right);
				}
			}
		}

		[NotifyParentProperty(true)]
		[DefaultValue(-1)]
		public int Bottom
		{
			get
			{
				return bottom;
			}
			set
			{
				if (bottom != value)
				{
					if (value < -1)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_7"), value, Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_8"));
					}
					bottom = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Bottom);
				}
			}
		}

		public UIElementMargins()
		{
		}

		public UIElementMargins(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
		{
			if (left < -1 || top < -1 || right < -1 || bottom < -1)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_9"));
			}
			this.left = left;
			this.top = top;
			this.right = right;
			this.bottom = bottom;
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			left = (right = (top = (bottom = -1)));
		}

		public object Clone()
		{
			return new UIElementMargins(left, top, right, bottom);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"Left = {Left}, Top = {Top}, Right = {Right}, Bottom = {Bottom}";
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is UIElementMargins))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			UIElementMargins uIElementMargins = (UIElementMargins)obj;
			if (left == uIElementMargins.left && top == uIElementMargins.top && right == uIElementMargins.right)
			{
				return bottom == uIElementMargins.bottom;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return base.GetHashCode();
		}

		public static Rectangle operator +(UIElementMargins margins, Rectangle rect)
		{
			return rect + margins;
		}

		public static Rectangle operator +(Rectangle rect, UIElementMargins margins)
		{
			if (margins == null)
			{
				return rect;
			}
			rect.X -= margins.left;
			rect.Width += margins.left + margins.right;
			rect.Y -= margins.top;
			rect.Height += margins.top + margins.bottom;
			return rect;
		}

		public static Rectangle operator -(UIElementMargins margins, Rectangle rect)
		{
			return rect - margins;
		}

		public static Rectangle operator -(Rectangle rect, UIElementMargins margins)
		{
			if (margins == null)
			{
				return rect;
			}
			rect.X += margins.left;
			rect.Width -= margins.left + margins.right;
			rect.Y += margins.top;
			rect.Height -= margins.top + margins.bottom;
			return rect;
		}

		public static bool operator ==(UIElementMargins margins, Rectangle rect)
		{
			try
			{
				return margins.left == rect.Left && margins.top == rect.Top && margins.right == rect.Right && margins.bottom == rect.Bottom;
			}
			catch
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static bool operator !=(UIElementMargins margins, Rectangle rect)
		{
			return !(margins == rect);
		}

		public static bool operator ==(Rectangle rect, UIElementMargins margins)
		{
			return margins == rect;
		}

		public static bool operator !=(Rectangle rect, UIElementMargins margins)
		{
			return !(margins == rect);
		}

		public static Rectangle Add(UIElementMargins margins, Rectangle rect)
		{
			return rect + margins;
		}

		public static Rectangle Add(Rectangle rect, UIElementMargins margins)
		{
			return rect + margins;
		}

		public static Rectangle Subtract(UIElementMargins margins, Rectangle rect)
		{
			return rect - margins;
		}

		public static Rectangle Subtract(Rectangle rect, UIElementMargins margins)
		{
			return rect - margins;
		}
	}
	[TypeConverter(typeof(ExpandableObjectConverter))]
	public class MarqueeInfo : SubObjectBase
	{
		private const MarqueeStyle StyleDefault = MarqueeStyle.Scrolling;

		private MarqueeStyle marqueeStyle;

		private const Direction DirectionDefault = Direction.RightToLeft;

		private Direction direction = Direction.RightToLeft;

		private const bool IsActiveDefault = false;

		private bool isActive;

		private const int ScrollAmountDefault = 1;

		private int scrollAmount = 1;

		private const int DelayMinimum = 1;

		private const int DelayDefault = 100;

		private int delay = 100;

		private Timer timer;

		private UltraStatusPanel panel;

		private string currentDisplayText;

		private string originalPanelText;

		private Point offset = Point.Empty;

		private bool isItemVisible = true;

		private EventHandler tickEventHandler;

		private Direction bounceDirection = Direction.RightToLeft;

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_MarqueeInfo_P_MarqueeStyle")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(MarqueeStyle.Scrolling)]
		public MarqueeStyle MarqueeStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return marqueeStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (marqueeStyle != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(MarqueeStyle), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(MarqueeStyle));
					}
					marqueeStyle = value;
					ResetMarquee();
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.MarqueeStyle);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_MarqueeInfo_P_MarqueeDirection")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Appearance")]
		[DefaultValue(Direction.RightToLeft)]
		public Direction MarqueeDirection
		{
			get
			{
				return direction;
			}
			set
			{
				if (direction != value)
				{
					if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(Direction), value))
					{
						throw new InvalidEnumArgumentException("value", (int)value, typeof(Direction));
					}
					direction = value;
					ResetMarquee();
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Direction);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_MarqueeInfo_P_IsActive")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		[DefaultValue(false)]
		public bool IsActive
		{
			get
			{
				if (IsValidObject)
				{
					return isActive;
				}
				return false;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(isActive && value) && (isActive || value))
				{
					isActive = value;
					ResetMarquee();
					if (value)
					{
						Start();
					}
					else
					{
						Stop();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_MarqueeInfo_P_MarqueeScrollAmount")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		[DefaultValue(1)]
		public int MarqueeScrollAmount
		{
			get
			{
				return scrollAmount;
			}
			set
			{
				if (scrollAmount != value)
				{
					if (value < 1)
					{
						throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
					}
					scrollAmount = value;
					NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.ScrollAmount);
				}
			}
		}

		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedDescription("LD_MarqueeInfo_P_Delay")]
		[Infragistics.Shared.LocalizedCategory("LC_Behavior")]
		[DefaultValue(100)]
		public int Delay
		{
			get
			{
				return delay;
			}
			set
			{
				if (delay == value)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (value < 1)
				{
					if (delay == 1)
					{
						return;
					}
					delay = 1;
				}
				else
				{
					delay = value;
				}
				if (timer != null)
				{
					timer.Interval = delay;
				}
				NotifyPropChange(StatusBarPropertyIds.Delay);
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public string CurrentText
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsValidObject)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				MarqueeStyle marqueeStyle = MarqueeStyle;
				if (marqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Typing)
				{
					if (!IsActive)
					{
						return panel.Text;
					}
					if (originalPanelText != panel.Text)
					{
						currentDisplayText = string.Empty;
					}
					return currentDisplayText;
				}
				return panel.Text;
			}
		}

		private Direction CurrentScrollDirection
		{
			get
			{
				if (MarqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Bouncing)
				{
					return bounceDirection;
				}
				return MarqueeDirection;
			}
		}

		private bool IsValidObject
		{
			get
			{
				if (panel != null && !panel.Disposed && !base.Disposed)
				{
					return panel.Style == PanelStyle.Marquee;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		internal bool IsItemVisible
		{
			get
			{
				if (MarqueeStyle != MarqueeStyle.Flashing)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return isItemVisible;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler TickEventHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (tickEventHandler == null)
				{
					tickEventHandler = OnTimerTick;
				}
				return tickEventHandler;
			}
		}

		public MarqueeInfo(UltraStatusPanel parentPanel)
		{
			if (parentPanel == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString("LE_V2_Exception_11"));
			}
			panel = parentPanel;
		}

		private void OnTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!IsActive)
			{
				Stop();
			}
			else
			{
				Step();
			}
		}

		private void StartTimer()
		{
			if (timer == null)
			{
				timer = Utilities.CreateTimer();
				timer.Tick += TickEventHandler;
			}
			if (timer.Interval != Delay)
			{
				timer.Interval = Delay;
			}
			if (!timer.Enabled)
			{
				timer.Start();
			}
		}

		private void StopTimer()
		{
			if (timer != null)
			{
				timer.Stop();
				timer.Tick -= TickEventHandler;
				timer.Dispose();
				timer = null;
			}
		}

		public void Start()
		{
			if (IsValidObject && panel.UltraStatusBar != null)
			{
				Stop();
				isActive = true;
				originalPanelText = panel.Text;
				switch (MarqueeStyle)
				{
				case MarqueeStyle.Scrolling:
				case MarqueeStyle.Sliding:
				case MarqueeStyle.Bouncing:
					InitializeOffset();
					break;
				case MarqueeStyle.Typing:
					currentDisplayText = string.Empty;
					break;
				case MarqueeStyle.Flashing:
					isItemVisible = true;
					break;
				}
				StartTimer();
			}
		}

		public void Stop()
		{
			StopTimer();
			isActive = false;
		}

		internal Rectangle GetDisplayRectangle(Rectangle clientRect)
		{
			if (!IsActive)
			{
				return clientRect;
			}
			switch (MarqueeStyle)
			{
			default:
			{
				Rectangle empty = Rectangle.Empty;
				Size imageAndTextSize = panel.ImageAndTextSize;
				return (CurrentScrollDirection != Direction.LeftToRight && CurrentScrollDirection != Direction.RightToLeft) ? new Rectangle(clientRect.X + offset.X, clientRect.Y + offset.Y, clientRect.Width, imageAndTextSize.Height) : new Rectangle(clientRect.X + offset.X, clientRect.Y + offset.Y, imageAndTextSize.Width, clientRect.Height);
			}
			case MarqueeStyle.Typing:
			case MarqueeStyle.Flashing:
				return clientRect;
			}
		}

		internal void ResetMarquee()
		{
			if (IsActive)
			{
				Stop();
				Start();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			Stop();
			base.OnDispose();
		}

		private void InitializeOffset()
		{
			if (!IsValidObject || MarqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Flashing || MarqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Typing)
			{
				return;
			}
			Direction marqueeDirection = MarqueeDirection;
			bool flag = MarqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Bouncing;
			if (flag)
			{
				bounceDirection = marqueeDirection;
			}
			UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
			if (uIElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Size size = uIElement.RectInsideBorders.Size;
			Size imageAndTextSize = panel.ImageAndTextSize;
			switch (marqueeDirection)
			{
			case Direction.RightToLeft:
				if (flag && imageAndTextSize.Width < size.Width)
				{
					offset = new Point(size.Width - imageAndTextSize.Width, 0);
				}
				else
				{
					offset = new Point(size.Width, 0);
				}
				break;
			case Direction.LeftToRight:
				if (flag && imageAndTextSize.Width < size.Width)
				{
					offset = new Point(0, 0);
				}
				else
				{
					offset = new Point(-imageAndTextSize.Width, 0);
				}
				break;
			case Direction.TopToBottom:
				if (flag && imageAndTextSize.Height < size.Height)
				{
					offset = new Point(0, 0);
				}
				else
				{
					offset = new Point(0, -imageAndTextSize.Height);
				}
				break;
			case Direction.BottomToTop:
				if (flag && imageAndTextSize.Height < size.Height)
				{
					offset = new Point(0, size.Height - imageAndTextSize.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					offset = new Point(0, size.Height);
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateOffset()
		{
			if (!IsValidObject || MarqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Flashing || MarqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Typing)
			{
				return;
			}
			UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
			Direction currentScrollDirection = CurrentScrollDirection;
			if (uIElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (currentScrollDirection)
			{
			case Direction.RightToLeft:
				offset.Offset(-scrollAmount, 0);
				break;
			case Direction.LeftToRight:
				offset.Offset(scrollAmount, 0);
				break;
			case Direction.TopToBottom:
				offset.Offset(0, scrollAmount);
				break;
			case Direction.BottomToTop:
				offset.Offset(0, -scrollAmount);
				break;
			}
			Rectangle rectInsideBorders = uIElement.RectInsideBorders;
			Rectangle displayRectangle = GetDisplayRectangle(rectInsideBorders);
			switch (MarqueeStyle)
			{
			case MarqueeStyle.Scrolling:
			{
				Rectangle rectangle = rectInsideBorders;
				rectangle.Inflate(1, 1);
				if (!rectangle.IntersectsWith(displayRectangle))
				{
					InitializeOffset();
				}
				break;
			}
			case MarqueeStyle.Sliding:
				switch (currentScrollDirection)
				{
				case Direction.RightToLeft:
					if (displayRectangle.Width > rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Right <= rectInsideBorders.Right)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Width <= rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Left <= rectInsideBorders.Left)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					break;
				case Direction.LeftToRight:
					if (displayRectangle.Width > rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Left >= rectInsideBorders.Left)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Width <= rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Right >= rectInsideBorders.Right)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					break;
				case Direction.TopToBottom:
					if (displayRectangle.Height > rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Top >= rectInsideBorders.Top)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Height <= rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Bottom >= rectInsideBorders.Bottom)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					break;
				case Direction.BottomToTop:
					if (displayRectangle.Height > rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Bottom <= rectInsideBorders.Bottom)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Height <= rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Top <= rectInsideBorders.Top)
					{
						Stop();
					}
					break;
				}
				break;
			case MarqueeStyle.Bouncing:
				switch (currentScrollDirection)
				{
				case Direction.RightToLeft:
					if (displayRectangle.Width > rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Right <= rectInsideBorders.Right)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.LeftToRight;
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Width <= rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Left <= rectInsideBorders.Left)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.LeftToRight;
					}
					break;
				case Direction.LeftToRight:
					if (displayRectangle.Width > rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Left >= rectInsideBorders.Left)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.RightToLeft;
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Width <= rectInsideBorders.Width && displayRectangle.Right >= rectInsideBorders.Right)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.RightToLeft;
					}
					break;
				case Direction.TopToBottom:
					if (displayRectangle.Height > rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Top >= rectInsideBorders.Top)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.BottomToTop;
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Height <= rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Bottom >= rectInsideBorders.Bottom)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.BottomToTop;
					}
					break;
				case Direction.BottomToTop:
					if (displayRectangle.Height > rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Bottom <= rectInsideBorders.Bottom)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.TopToBottom;
					}
					else if (displayRectangle.Height <= rectInsideBorders.Height && displayRectangle.Top <= rectInsideBorders.Top)
					{
						bounceDirection = Direction.TopToBottom;
					}
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void Step()
		{
			if (!IsValidObject)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (MarqueeStyle)
			{
			case MarqueeStyle.Scrolling:
			case MarqueeStyle.Sliding:
			case MarqueeStyle.Bouncing:
				UpdateOffset();
				break;
			case MarqueeStyle.Typing:
			{
				if (panel.Text == null || panel.Text.Length == 0)
				{
					currentDisplayText = string.Empty;
					break;
				}
				int num = ((CurrentText != null) ? CurrentText.Length : 0);
				int length = panel.Text.Length;
				int length2 = ((num < length) ? (num + 1) : 0);
				currentDisplayText = panel.Text.Substring(0, length2);
				break;
			}
			case MarqueeStyle.Flashing:
				isItemVisible = !isItemVisible;
				break;
			}
			UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)(object)panel.UIElement;
			if (uIElement != null)
			{
				uIElement.DirtyChildElements();
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			if (MarqueeStyle == MarqueeStyle.Scrolling && Delay == 100 && MarqueeDirection == Direction.RightToLeft && MarqueeScrollAmount == 1)
			{
				return IsActive;
			}
			return true;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void Reset()
		{
			IsActive = false;
			MarqueeStyle = MarqueeStyle.Scrolling;
			Delay = 100;
			MarqueeDirection = Direction.RightToLeft;
			MarqueeScrollAmount = 1;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"MarqueeInfo [{MarqueeStyle}], Direction = {MarqueeDirection}";
		}
	}

		internal static bool IsDWMCompositionEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				if (Environment.OSVersion.Version.Major != 6 || Environment.OSVersion.Version.Minor > 1)
				{
					return false;
				}
				try
				{
					return IsDWMCompositionEnabledUnsafe();
				}
				catch
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		[DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
		private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr hWnd, int msg, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam);

		[DllImport("user32.dll")]

		[DllImport("user32")]
		private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr hwnd, int wMsg, ref POINT wParam, IntPtr lParam);

		private static extern IntPtr SendMessage(IntPtr hwnd, int wMsg, ref GETTEXTEX wParam, StringBuilder lParam);

		private static extern int DwmIsCompositionEnabled([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] out bool enabled);

		internal static int GetLineIndex(int lineNumber, RichTextBox richText)
		{
			if (richText == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return SendMessage(richText.Handle, 187, new IntPtr(lineNumber), IntPtr.Zero).ToInt32();
		}

		internal static IntPtr StartContainerResize(Control container, int hitTestCode)
		{
			try
			{
				return StartContainerResize(container.Handle, hitTestCode);
			}
			catch
			{
				return new IntPtr(-1);
			}
		}

		internal static IntPtr GetWindowHandle(Point point)
		{
			try
			{
				return GetWindowHandleUnsafe(point);
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
			}
			return IntPtr.Zero;
		}

		internal static string GetText(RichTextBox rtf)
		{
			if (rtf == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (rtf.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				try
				{
					return GetTextUnsafe(rtf);
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return rtf.Text;
		}

		internal static int GetTextLength(RichTextBox rtf)
		{
			if (rtf == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (rtf.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				try
				{
					SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
					if (Utilities.CanAssert)
					{
						securityPermission.Assert();
					}
					return GetTextLengthUnsafe(rtf);
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return rtf.TextLength;
		}

		internal static Point GetPositionFromCharIndex(RichTextBox rtf, int index)
		{
			if (rtf == null)
			{
				return Point.Empty;
			}
			if (rtf.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				try
				{
					if (index < 0 || index > GetTextLengthUnsafe(rtf))
					{
						return Point.Empty;
					}
					return GetPositionFromCharIndexUnsafe(rtf, index);
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return rtf.GetPositionFromCharIndex(index);
		}

		internal static int GetCharIndexFromPosition(RichTextBox rtf, Point position)
		{
			if (rtf == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (rtf.IsHandleCreated)
			{
				try
				{
					return GetCharIndexFromPositionUnsafe(rtf, position);
				}
				catch (SecurityException)
				{
				}
			}
			return rtf.GetCharIndexFromPosition(position);
		}

		internal static string[] GetLines(RichTextBox rtf)
		{
			string text = GetText(rtf);
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			int num = 0;
			while (num < text.Length)
			{
				int i;
				for (i = num; i < text.Length; i++)
				{
					char c = text[i];
					if (c == '\r' || c == '\n' || c == '\f')
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				string value = text.Substring(num, i - num);
				arrayList.Add(value);
				if (i < text.Length && text[i] == '\f')
				{
					i++;
				}
				else
				{
					if (i < text.Length && text[i] == '\r')
					{
						i++;
					}
					if (i < text.Length && text[i] == '\n')
					{
						i++;
					}
				}
				num = i;
			}
			if (text.Length > 0 && (text[text.Length - 1] == '\r' || text[text.Length - 1] == '\n' || text[text.Length - 1] == '\f'))
			{
				arrayList.Add("");
			}
			return (string[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(string));
		}

		internal static int GetWindowLongApi(IntPtr hwnd, int nIndex)
		{
			return GetWindowLong(hwnd, nIndex);
		}

		[DllImport("user32", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
		private static extern int GetWindowLong(IntPtr hwnd, int nIndex);

		private static IntPtr StartContainerResize(IntPtr hWnd, int hitTestCode)
		{
			return SendMessage(hWnd, 161, new IntPtr(hitTestCode), IntPtr.Zero);
		}

		private static IntPtr GetWindowHandleUnsafe(Point point)
		{
			return WindowFromPoint(new POINT
			{
				x = point.X,
				y = point.Y
			});
		}

		private static int GetCharIndexFromPositionUnsafe(RichTextBox rtf, Point position)
		{
			POINT lParam = new POINT
			{
				x = position.X,
				y = position.Y
			};
			int num = (int)SendMessage(rtf.Handle, 215, IntPtr.Zero, ref lParam).ToInt64();
			int textLengthUnsafe = GetTextLengthUnsafe(rtf);
			if (num > textLengthUnsafe)
			{
				num = Math.Max(textLengthUnsafe - 1, 0);
			}
			return num;
		}

		private static Point GetPositionFromCharIndexUnsafe(RichTextBox rtf, int index)
		{
			POINT wParam = default(POINT);
			SendMessage(rtf.Handle, 214, ref wParam, new IntPtr(index));
			return new Point(wParam.x, wParam.y);
		}

		private static int GetTextLengthUnsafe(RichTextBox rtf)
		{
			GETTEXTLENGTHEX wParam = new GETTEXTLENGTHEX
			{
				flags = 0,
				codepage = ((Marshal.SystemDefaultCharSize != 1) ? 1200 : 0)
			};
			return (int)SendMessage(rtf.Handle, 1119, ref wParam, IntPtr.Zero).ToInt64();
		}

		private static string GetTextUnsafe(RichTextBox rtf)
		{
			int textLengthUnsafe = GetTextLengthUnsafe(rtf);
			if (textLengthUnsafe == -2147024809)
			{
				return null;
			}
			textLengthUnsafe++;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(textLengthUnsafe);
			GETTEXTEX wParam = new GETTEXTEX
			{
				cb = textLengthUnsafe * Marshal.SystemDefaultCharSize,
				flags = 0,
				codepage = ((Marshal.SystemDefaultCharSize != 1) ? 1200 : 0),
				lpDefaultChar = IntPtr.Zero,
				lpUsedDefChar = IntPtr.Zero
			};
			int num = (int)SendMessage(rtf.Handle, 1118, ref wParam, stringBuilder).ToInt64();
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static bool IsDWMCompositionEnabledUnsafe()
		{
			bool enabled;
			int num = DwmIsCompositionEnabled(out enabled);
			return num == 0 && enabled;
		}

	public class PanelSplitterUIElement : SplitterUIElement
	{
		private int lastDelta = -1;

		public override UIElementBorderStyle BorderStyle => (UIElementBorderStyle)1;

		public UltraStatusPanel Panel => ((UIElement)this).PrimaryContext as UltraStatusPanel;

		private UltraStatusBar UltraStatusBar => ((UIElement)this).Control as UltraStatusBar;

		protected override bool DrawAdjustmentBarWhileSizing => UltraStatusBar.ResizeStyle == ResizeStyle.Deferred;

		public override UIRole UIRole => StyleUtils.GetRole(Panel, StyleUtils.Role.PanelSplitter);

		public PanelSplitterUIElement(UIElement parent, UltraStatusPanel panel)
			: base(parent, true)
		{
			if (panel == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			((UIElement)this).PrimaryContext = panel;
		}

		protected internal void Initialize(UltraStatusPanel panel)
		{
			if (panel == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			((UIElement)this).PrimaryContext = panel;
		}

		public override void GetAdjustmentRange(Point point, out UIElementAdjustmentRangeParams range)
		{
			((SplitterUIElement)this).GetAdjustmentRange(point, ref range);
			range.leftRightAdjustmentBar.Width = UltraStatusBar.InterPanelSpacing;
			if (((UIElement)((UIElement)this).ControlElement).GetDescendant(typeof(PanelUIElement), (object)Panel) is PanelUIElement panelUIElement)
			{
				range.maxDeltaLeft = -(((UIElement)panelUIElement).Rect.Width - Panel.MinWidth);
			}
		}

		public override void ApplyAdjustment(Point delta)
		{
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
			if (ultraStatusBar != null && panel != null)
			{
				if (ultraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.Immediate)
				{
					panel.Width += delta.X;
				}
				PanelEventArgs e = new PanelEventArgs(panel);
				ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.AfterDragResize, e);
				if (ultraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.Immediate)
				{
					((SplitterUIElement)this).ApplyAdjustment(delta);
				}
			}
		}

		public override Cursor GetAdjustableCursor(Point point)
		{
			return Cursors.VSplit;
		}

		protected override void OnNewDeltaX(int newDeltaX)
		{
			((AdjustableUIElement)this).OnNewDeltaX(newDeltaX);
			if (UltraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.Deferred && newDeltaX != lastDelta)
			{
				if (lastDelta == -1)
				{
					Panel.Width += newDeltaX;
				}
				else
				{
					Panel.Width += newDeltaX - lastDelta;
				}
				lastDelta = newDeltaX;
				((UIElement)this).Parent.DirtyChildElements();
				((UIElement)this).Control.Update();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnElementAdjustmentStart(bool isUpDownAdjustment, bool initialUpDownAdjustmentPointInBottomBorder, bool isLeftRightAdjustment, bool initialLeftRightAdjustmentPointInRightBorder)
		{
			lastDelta = -1;
			((AdjustableUIElement)this).OnElementAdjustmentStart(isUpDownAdjustment, initialUpDownAdjustmentPointInBottomBorder, isLeftRightAdjustment, initialLeftRightAdjustmentPointInRightBorder);
		}

		protected override void InitAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
		}

		protected override bool DrawTheme(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected override void OnDoubleClick(bool adjustableArea)
		{
			if (adjustableArea && ((UIElement)this).Adjustable)
			{
				UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
				UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
				if (ultraStatusBar != null && panel != null)
				{
					CancelablePanelEventArgs e = new CancelablePanelEventArgs(panel);
					ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.BeforeAutoSizePanel, e);
					if (!e.Cancel)
					{
						panel.Width = panel.AutoSizeWidth;
						ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.AfterAutoSizePanel, new PanelEventArgs(panel));
					}
				}
			}
			((AdjustableUIElement)this).OnDoubleClick(adjustableArea);
		}

		protected override void DrawBackColor(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
		}

		protected override bool CanStartAdjustment(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = UltraStatusBar;
			UltraStatusPanel panel = Panel;
			if (ultraStatusBar == null || panel == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			CancelablePanelEventArgs e2 = new CancelablePanelEventArgs(panel);
			ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.BeforeDragResize, e2);
			return !e2.Cancel;
		}
	}

					((UIRoleCache)(ref roleFillCache)).Reset();
				}
			}
		}
