		protected bool ShouldSerializeAppearance()
		{
			if (HasAppearance)
			{
				return appearanceHolder.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return false;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void ResetAppearance()
		{
			if (HasAppearance)
			{
				appearanceHolder.Reset();
				NotifyPropChange((Enum)(object)(ProgressBarPropertyIds)6);
			}
		}

		protected bool ShouldSerializeFillAppearance()
		{
			if (HasFillAppearance)
			{
				return fillAppearanceHolder.ShouldSerialize();
			}
			return false;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void ResetFillAppearance()
		{
			if (HasFillAppearance)
			{
				fillAppearanceHolder.Reset();
				NotifyPropChange((Enum)(object)(ProgressBarPropertyIds)7);
			}
		}

		protected internal void InitAppearances(AppearancesCollection appearances)
		{
			this.appearances = appearances;
			if (fillAppearanceHolder != null)
			{
				fillAppearanceHolder.Collection = appearances;
			}
			if (appearanceHolder != null)
			{
				appearanceHolder.Collection = appearances;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			if (HasFillAppearance)
			{
				fillAppearanceHolder.SubObjectPropChanged -= base.SubObjectPropChangeHandler;
				fillAppearanceHolder = null;
			}
			base.OnDispose();
		}

		protected override void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo)
		{
			if (HasAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == appearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange((Enum)(object)(ProgressBarPropertyIds)6, propChangeInfo);
			}
			else if (HasFillAppearance && propChangeInfo.Source == fillAppearanceHolder.RootAppearance)
			{
				NotifyPropChange((Enum)(object)(ProgressBarPropertyIds)7, propChangeInfo);
			}
			base.OnSubObjectPropChanged(propChangeInfo);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerialize()
		{
			//IL_002b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0031: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (Minimum == 0 && Maximum == 100 && !ShouldSerializeAppearance() && !ShouldSerializeFillAppearance() && Value == 0 && (int)Style == -1 && "[Formatted]".Equals(Label) && "P0".Equals(PercentFormat) && ShowLabel)
			{
				return SegmentWidth != -1;
			}
			return true;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void Reset()
		{
			Minimum = 0;
			Maximum = 100;
			ResetAppearance();
			ResetFillAppearance();
			Value = 0;
			Style = (ProgressBarStyle)(-1);
			Label = "[Formatted]";
			PercentFormat = "P0";
			ShowLabel = true;
			SegmentWidth = -1;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			return $"ProgressBarInfo [{Style}], Min = {Minimum}, Max = {Maximum}";
		}

		void IProgressBarInfo.ResolveAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			UltraProgressBar.ResolveAppearance((IProgressBarInfo)(object)this, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
		}

		{
			UltraProgressBar.ResolveFillAppearance((IProgressBarInfo)(object)this, ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
		}

	public class SizeGripUIElement : UIElement

		public class SizeGripAccessibleObject : UIElementAccessibleObject

			}
		}

		public const int DefaultWidth = 16;

		private AccessibleObject accessibleObject;

		public UltraStatusBar StatusBar => ((UIElement)this).Control as UltraStatusBar;

		public override Cursor Cursor
		{
			get
			{
				if (StatusBar != null && StatusBar.CanResizeContainer())
				{
					return Cursors.SizeNWSE;
				}
				return ((UIElement)this).Cursor;
			}
		}

		public override bool IsAccessibleElement => true;

		public override AccessibleObject AccessibilityInstance
		{
			get
			{
				if (accessibleObject == null)
				{
					accessibleObject = StatusBar.CreateAccessibilityInstance(this);
				}
				return accessibleObject;
			}
		}

		public override UIRole UIRole => StyleUtils.GetRole(StatusBar, StyleUtils.Role.SizeGrip);

		public SizeGripUIElement(UIElement parent)
			: base(parent)
		{
		}

		protected override bool DrawTheme(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			if (!XPThemes.StatusBar.DrawStatusBarItem((StatusBarItem)1, ref drawParams, ((UIElement)this).Rect, ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).InvalidRect))
			{
				return false;
			}
			XPThemes.StatusBar.DrawStatusBarItem((StatusBarItem)2, ref drawParams, ((UIElement)this).Rect, ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).InvalidRect);
			return true;
		}

		protected override void DrawForeground(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			//IL_0022: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ViewStyle viewStyle = StatusBar?.ViewStyleResolved ?? ViewStyle.Standard;
			if (viewStyle == ViewStyle.Office2003 || viewStyle == ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005 || viewStyle == ViewStyle.Office2007)
			{
				DrawGripper(drawParams);
				return;
			}
			Color alphaBlendColor = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetAlphaBlendColor(((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).GetBackColor(((UIElement)this).Enabled));
			ControlPaint.DrawSizeGrip(((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics, alphaBlendColor, ((UIElement)this).Rect);
		}

		private bool ResizeForm(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((e.Button & MouseButtons.Left) == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(((UIElement)this).Control is UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!ultraStatusBar.CanResizeContainer())
			{
				return false;
			}
			CancelEventArgs e2 = new CancelEventArgs();
			ultraStatusBar.FireEvent(StatusBarEventIds.BeforeDragSizeGrip, e2);
			if (e2.Cancel)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Control parent = ((UIElement)this).Control.Parent;
			((UIElement)this).Control.Capture = false;
			Cursor.Current = Cursors.SizeNWSE;
			IntPtr intPtr = NativeWindowMethods.StartContainerResize(parent, 17);
			return intPtr == IntPtr.Zero;
		}

		protected override bool OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e, bool adjustableArea, ref UIElement captureMouseForElement)
		{
			if (ResizeForm(e))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return ((UIElement)this).OnMouseDown(e, adjustableArea, ref captureMouseForElement);
		}

		public override AccessibleObject Navigate(AccessibleNavigation navdir)
		{
			if (navdir == AccessibleNavigation.Left || navdir == AccessibleNavigation.Previous)
			{
				UltraStatusBar statusBar = StatusBar;
				if (statusBar != null)
				{
					return statusBar.Panels[statusBar.Panels.Count - 1].AccessibilityObject;
				}
			}
			return ((UIElement)this).Navigate(navdir);
		}

		private void DrawGripper(UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			bool flag = StatusBar.ViewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.Office2007;
			Color color = (flag ? Office2007ColorTable.Colors.StatusBarGrabHandleDarkSquare : StatusBar.MSColors.GrabHandleDarkSquare);
			Color color2 = (flag ? Office2007ColorTable.Colors.StatusBarGrabHandleLightSquare : Color.White);
			Rectangle rectInsideBorders = ((UIElement)this).RectInsideBorders;
			Brush brush = Utilities.CreateSolidBrush(color);
			Brush brush2 = Utilities.CreateSolidBrush(color2);
			int x = rectInsideBorders.X;
			int y = rectInsideBorders.Y;
			int num = 4;
			int num2 = rectInsideBorders.Width / num;
			int num3 = rectInsideBorders.Height / num;
			x = rectInsideBorders.Right - num - 1;
			y = rectInsideBorders.Bottom - num - 1;
			int num4 = 1;
			int num5 = 3;
			while (num5 > 0)
			{
				((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics.FillRectangle(brush2, x + 1, y + 1, 2, 2);
				((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics.FillRectangle(brush, x, y, 2, 2);
				y -= 4;
				num4++;
				if (num4 > num5 || y < rectInsideBorders.Top + 1)
				{
					num4 = 1;
					num5--;
					x -= 4;
					y = rectInsideBorders.Bottom - num - 1;
				}
			}
			brush.Dispose();
		}

		protected override void InitAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			//IL_001a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			UltraStatusBar statusBar = StatusBar;
			if (statusBar != null)
			{
				UIRole uIRole = ((UIElement)this).UIRole;
				if (uIRole != null)
				{
					ResolutionOrderInfo resolutionOrderInfo = ComponentRole.GetResolutionOrderInfo(((UIElement)this).GetComponentRole());
					if (((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleBefore || ((ResolutionOrderInfo)(ref resolutionOrderInfo)).UseStyleAfter)
					{
						uIRole.ResolveAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps, (RoleState)1);
					}
				}
			}
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 0x10000uL) != 0L)
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColorAlpha = (Alpha)3;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps ^ 0x10000uL);
			}
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 0x80000000uL) != 0L && statusBar.ViewStyleResolved != ViewStyle.Standard)
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ThemedElementAlpha = (Alpha)3;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps ^ 0x80000000uL);
			}
			if (((ulong)requestedProps & 0x100uL) != 0L)
			{
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps ^ 0x100uL);
			}
			else if (((ulong)requestedProps & 0x20000uL) != 0L)
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ForegroundAlpha = (Alpha)3;
				requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps ^ 0x20000uL);
			}
			((UIElement)this).InitAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
		}

		protected override void DrawImage(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			Image image = ((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).GetImage((ImageList)null);
			((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).DrawImage(image, base.rectValue, false, (ImageAttributes)null);
		}

	public class TimerManager
	{
		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Timer timer;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static EventHandler timeElapsedDelegate;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static EventHandler tickHandler;

		public static event EventHandler TimeElapsed
		{
			add
			{
				AddEventHandler(value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveEventHandler(value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (timeElapsedDelegate != null)
			{
				timeElapsedDelegate(null, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		private static void AddEventHandler(EventHandler handler)
		{
			lock (typeof(TimerManager))
			{
				if (timeElapsedDelegate == null)
				{
					if (timer == null)
					{
						timer = Utilities.CreateTimer();
						if (tickHandler == null)
						{
							tickHandler = OnTick;
						}
						timer.Tick += tickHandler;
					}
					timer.Interval = 500;
					timer.Start();
					timeElapsedDelegate = handler;
				}
				else
				{
					timeElapsedDelegate = (EventHandler)Delegate.Combine(timeElapsedDelegate, handler);
				}
			}
		}

		private static void RemoveEventHandler(EventHandler handler)
		{
			lock (typeof(TimerManager))
			{
				if (timeElapsedDelegate != null)
				{
					timeElapsedDelegate = (EventHandler)Delegate.Remove(timeElapsedDelegate, handler);
				}
				if (timeElapsedDelegate == null && timer != null)
				{
					timer.Stop();
					timer.Tick -= tickHandler;
					timer.Dispose();
					timer = null;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class UltraStatusBarBorderAreaResizeUIElement : UIElement
	{
		private int hitTestCode;

		private UltraStatusBar statusBar;

		public override Cursor Cursor
		{
			get
			{
				UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = StatusBar;
				if (ultraStatusBar != null && ultraStatusBar.IsFormRounded && ultraStatusBar.CanResizeContainer())
				{
					switch (hitTestCode)
					{
					case 10:
					case 11:
						return Cursors.SizeWE;
					case 17:
						return Cursors.SizeNWSE;
					case 15:
						return Cursors.SizeNS;
					case 16:
						return Cursors.SizeNESW;
					}
				}
				return ((UIElement)this).Cursor;
			}
		}

					case 13:

					case 16:
						return Border3DSide.Left | Border3DSide.Bottom;
					case 17:
						return Border3DSide.Top | Border3DSide.Right;
					case 12:
						return Border3DSide.Top;
					case 15:

		private bool IsCorner
		{
			get

				case 14:
				case 16:
				case 17:
					return true;
				default:
					return false;
				}
			}

		public override bool Contains(Point point, bool ignoreClipping)
		{
			bool flag = ((UIElement)this).Contains(point, ignoreClipping);
			if (!flag || !((UIElement)this).ClipSelf)
			{
				return flag;
			}
			Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
			Rectangle rectangle = rect;
			Rectangle rectangle2 = new Rectangle(rect.Location, new Size(2, 2));
			switch (hitTestCode)
			{
			case 13:
				rectangle.Offset(3, 3);
				break;
			case 14:
				rectangle.Offset(-3, 3);
				rectangle2.Offset(rectangle2.Left - rectangle2.Width, 0);
				break;
			case 16:
				rectangle.Offset(3, -3);
				rectangle2.Offset(0, rectangle2.Top - rectangle2.Height);
				break;
			case 17:
				rectangle.Offset(-3, -3);
				rectangle2.Offset(rectangle2.Left - rectangle2.Width, rectangle2.Top - rectangle2.Height);
				break;
			default:
				return flag;
			}
			if (rectangle.Contains(point))
			{
				return rectangle2.Contains(point);
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected override void DrawBorders(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			//IL_0019: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0020: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0023: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0031: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0036: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0025: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0028: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_003d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0040: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0045: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0080: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0085: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0099: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009f: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			if (statusBar.ToolbarsManager == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			FormDisplayStyle formDisplayStyle = statusBar.ToolbarsManager.FormDisplayStyle;
			if ((int)formDisplayStyle != 1 && (int)formDisplayStyle != 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			ToolbarFormBorderStyle toolbarFormBorderStyle = statusBar.ToolbarFormBorderStyle;
			if ((int)toolbarFormBorderStyle != 1 && (int)toolbarFormBorderStyle != 5 && (int)toolbarFormBorderStyle != 6)
			{
				((UIElement)this).DrawBorders(ref drawParams);
				return;
			}
			if (statusBar.ParentForm == null || !statusBar.IsDockedBottom)
			{
				((UIElement)this).DrawBorders(ref drawParams);
				return;
			}
			Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
			bool isCorner = IsCorner;
			AppearanceData appearanceData = drawParams.AppearanceData;
			Graphics graphics = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).Graphics;
			if (isCorner)
			{
				if ((int)statusBar.ToolbarFormBorderStyle == 6)
				{
					return;
				}
				using Pen pen = new Pen(((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetAlphaBlendColor(((AppearanceData)(ref appearanceData)).BorderColor));
				using Pen pen2 = new Pen(((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).GetAlphaBlendColor(((AppearanceData)(ref appearanceData)).BorderColor2));
				int num = 8;
				float num2 = 0f;
				float num3 = 0f;

				Rectangle rect3 = rect;

				rect2.Height = num;
				rect3.Width = num - 2;
				rect3.Height = num - 2;
				SmoothingMode smoothingMode = graphics.SmoothingMode;
				graphics.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
				switch (hitTestCode)
				{
				case 16:
					num2 = 90f;
					num3 = 90f;
					rect2.X = rect.Left;
					rect2.Y = rect.Bottom - (num + 1);
					break;
				case 17:
					num2 = 0f;
					num3 = 90f;
					rect2.X = rect.Right - (num + 1);
					rect2.Y = rect.Bottom - (num + 1);
					break;
				default:
					graphics.SmoothingMode = smoothingMode;
					return;
				}
				rect3.X = rect2.X + 1;

				return;
			}

			using (Brush brush = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).CreateSolidBrush(((AppearanceData)(ref appearanceData)).BackColor))
			{
				graphics.FillRectangle(brush, rect);
			}
			using Brush brush2 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).CreateSolidBrush(((AppearanceData)(ref appearanceData)).BorderColor);
			switch (hitTestCode)
			{
			case 15:
				graphics.FillRectangle(brush2, new Rectangle(rect.Left, rect.Bottom - 1, rect.Width, 1));
				rect.Offset(0, rect.Height - 2);
				rect.Height = 1;

				rect4.Offset(0, -1);
				break;
			case 10:
				graphics.FillRectangle(brush2, new Rectangle(rect.Left, rect.Top, 1, rect.Height));
				rect.X++;
				rect.Width = 1;
				rect4 = rect;
				rect4.Offset(1, 0);
				break;
			case 11:
				graphics.FillRectangle(brush2, new Rectangle(rect.Right - 1, rect.Top, 1, rect.Height));
				rect.Offset(rect.Width - 2, 0);
				rect.Width = 1;
				rect4 = rect;

			graphics.FillRectangle(brush3, rect);
			using Brush brush4 = ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).CreateSolidBrush(((AppearanceData)(ref appearanceData)).BorderColor3DBase);
			graphics.FillRectangle(brush4, rect4);
		}

		protected override bool DrawTheme(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			//IL_0018: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0037: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0073: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0077: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_007e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			IToolbarsManager toolbarsManager = statusBar.ToolbarsManager;
			if (toolbarsManager == null)
			{
				return ((UIElement)this).DrawTheme(ref drawParams);
			}
			if ((int)toolbarsManager.ToolbarFormBorderStyle != 2)
			{
				return ((UIElement)this).DrawTheme(ref drawParams);
			}
			bool isFormActive = toolbarsManager.IsFormActive;
			Rectangle rect = ((UIElement)this).Rect;
			FramePart val = (FramePart)0;
			switch (hitTestCode)
			{
			case 12:
			case 13:
			case 14:
			case 16:
			case 17:
				return true;
			case 15:
				val = (FramePart)2;
				break;
			case 10:
				val = (FramePart)0;
				break;
			case 11:
				val = (FramePart)1;
				break;
			}
			XPThemes.Window.DrawWindowFrame(false, val, isFormActive, ref drawParams, rect, ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).InvalidRect);
			return true;
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public override int GetNonClientHitTestCode(Point point)
		{
			if ((StatusBar?.IsFormRounded ?? false) && ((UIElement)this).Contains(point))
			{
				return hitTestCode;
			}
			return 1;
		}

		protected override void InitAppearance(ref AppearanceData appearance, ref AppearancePropFlags requestedProps)
		{
			((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BackColorAlpha = (Alpha)3;
			((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).ImageAlpha = (Alpha)3;
			requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps ^ 0x10000uL);
			requestedProps = (AppearancePropFlags)((ulong)requestedProps ^ 0x80000000uL);
			if (statusBar.ToolbarsManager != null)
			{
				statusBar.ToolbarsManager.ResolveFormBorderAppearance(ref appearance, ref requestedProps);
			}
			else
			{
				((AppearanceData)(ref appearance)).BorderAlpha = (Alpha)3;
			}
		}

		protected override bool OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e, bool adjustableArea, ref UIElement captureMouseForElement)
		{
			if (ResizeForm(e))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return ((UIElement)this).OnMouseDown(e, adjustableArea, ref captureMouseForElement);
		}

		private Rectangle AdjustRectInner(Rectangle rect)
		{
			return hitTestCode switch
			{
				10 => new Rectangle(rect.X + 1, rect.Y, 1, rect.Height), 
				11 => new Rectangle(rect.Left - 1, rect.Y, 1, rect.Height), 
				15 => new Rectangle(rect.X, rect.Top - 1, rect.Width, 1), 
				_ => rect, 
			};
		}

		private bool ResizeForm(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((e.Button & MouseButtons.Left) == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			UltraStatusBar ultraStatusBar = StatusBar;
			if (ultraStatusBar == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!ultraStatusBar.CanResizeContainer())
			{
				return false;
			}
			Control parent = ((UIElement)this).Control.Parent;
			((UIElement)this).Control.Capture = false;
			Cursor.Current = ((UIElement)this).Cursor;
			IntPtr intPtr = NativeWindowMethods.StartContainerResize(parent, hitTestCode);
			return intPtr == IntPtr.Zero;
		}
	}

		private void AddPanelSplitterHelper(UltraStatusPanel panel, UIElementsCollection oldElements, ref Rectangle workRect, int width)
		{
			width = Math.Min(width, workRect.Width);
			if (width <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (UltraStatusBar.ResizeStyle != ResizeStyle.None && panel != null && panel.SizingMode == PanelSizingMode.Adjustable)
			{
				PanelSplitterUIElement panelSplitterUIElement = ExtractExistingElementWithContext(oldElements, typeof(PanelSplitterUIElement), panel) as PanelSplitterUIElement;
				if (panelSplitterUIElement == null)
				{
					panelSplitterUIElement = new PanelSplitterUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, panel);
				}
				else
				{
					panelSplitterUIElement.Initialize(panel);
				}
				((UIElement)panelSplitterUIElement).Rect = new Rectangle(workRect.X, workRect.Y, width, workRect.Height);
				((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)panelSplitterUIElement);
			}
			workRect.Width -= width;
			workRect.X += width;
		}

		private UIElement ExtractExistingElementWithContext(UIElementsCollection oldElements, Type elementType, object context)
		{
			if (context == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Type type = context.GetType();
			for (int i = 0; i < ((ArrayList)(object)oldElements).Count; i++)
			{
				UIElement val = oldElements[i];
				if (val != null && !(((object)val).GetType() != elementType) && val.GetContext(type) == context)
				{
					oldElements[i] = null;
					return val;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void AddPanelHelper(UltraStatusPanel panel, UIElementsCollection oldElements, ref Rectangle workRect, int width)
		{
			PanelUIElement panelUIElement = ExtractExistingElementWithContext(oldElements, typeof(PanelUIElement), panel) as PanelUIElement;
			if (panelUIElement == null)
			{
				panelUIElement = new PanelUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, panel);
			}
			else
			{
				panelUIElement.Initialize(panel);
			}
			width = Math.Min(width, workRect.Width);
			if (panel.Style == PanelStyle.Marquee && ((UIElement)panelUIElement).Rect.Width != width)
			{
				panel.DirtyText();
			}
			((UIElement)panelUIElement).Rect = new Rectangle(workRect.X, workRect.Y, width, workRect.Height);
			((UIElement)this).ChildElements.Add((UIElement)(object)panelUIElement);
			workRect.Width -= width;
			workRect.X += width;
		}

		protected override bool DrawTheme(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			if (UltraStatusBar != null && UltraStatusBar.ViewStyleResolved != ViewStyle.Standard)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (((AppearanceData)(ref drawParams.AppearanceData)).GetBackColor(((UIElement)this).Enabled) == Color.Transparent)
			{
				return XPThemes.StatusBar.DrawStatusBarItem((StatusBarItem)3, ref drawParams, ((UIElement)this).Rect, ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).InvalidRect);
			}
			((UIElement)this).DrawBackColor(ref drawParams);
			return XPThemes.StatusBar.DrawStatusBarItem((StatusBarItem)1, ref drawParams, ((UIElement)this).Rect, ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).InvalidRect);
		}

		protected override void DrawBackColor(ref UIElementDrawParams drawParams)
		{
			if (UltraStatusBar != null && UltraStatusBar.ViewStyleResolved == ViewStyle.VisualStudio2005)
			{
				Blend blend = new Blend();
				blend.Factors = new float[5] { 0f, 0.1f, 0.35f, 1f, 1f };
				blend.Positions = new float[5] { 0f, 0.5f, 0.6f, 0.95f, 1f };
				((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).DrawBackColor(ref drawParams.AppearanceData, ((UIElement)this).RectInsideBorders, ((UIElementDrawParams)(ref drawParams)).InvalidRect, ((UIElement)this).Enabled, blend);
			}
			else
			{
				((UIElement)this).DrawBackColor(ref drawParams);
			}
		}

		private void InitBorderElement(int index, int hitTestCode, Rectangle rect)
		{
			if (borderResizeElements == null)
			{
				borderResizeElements = new UltraStatusBarBorderAreaResizeUIElement[5];
			}
			if (borderResizeElements[index] == null)
			{
				borderResizeElements[index] = new UltraStatusBarBorderAreaResizeUIElement((UIElement)(object)this, StatusBar, hitTestCode);
			}
			((UIElement)borderResizeElements[index]).Rect = rect;
		}
	}

namespace Infragistics.Win.UltraWinStatusBar.Design
{
	public class PanelControlConverter : ReferenceConverter
	{
		public PanelControlConverter(Type type)
			: base(type)
		{
		}

		protected override bool IsValueAllowed(ITypeDescriptorContext context, object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!(value is Control control))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (context == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(context.Instance is UltraStatusPanel { UltraStatusBar: not null, Style: var style } ultraStatusPanel))
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (style)
			{
			case PanelStyle.CharacterPosition:
				return control is RichTextBox;
			case PanelStyle.ControlContainer:
			{
				if (control is Form)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if ((object)control == ultraStatusPanel.UltraStatusBar)
				{
					return false;
				}
				for (Control parent = ((Control)(object)ultraStatusPanel.UltraStatusBar).Parent; parent != null; parent = parent.Parent)
				{
					if (control == parent)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Shared
{
	internal sealed class SR
	{
		private static Infragistics.Shared.SR loader;

		private ResourceManager[] resources;

		private SR()
		{
			string text = "Infragistics.Win.UltraWinStatusBar.strings";
			string[] array = text.Split(';');
			int num = 0;
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text2 in array2)
			{
				if (text2 != null && text2.Length > 0)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			resources = new ResourceManager[num];
			Assembly assembly = GetType().Assembly;
			int num2 = 0;
			string[] array3 = array;
			foreach (string text3 in array3)
			{
				if (text3 != null && text3.Length > 0)
				{
					resources[num2++] = new ResourceManager(text3, assembly);
				}
			}
		}

		private static Infragistics.Shared.SR GetLoader()
		{
			if (loader == null)
			{
				lock (typeof(Infragistics.Shared.SR))
				{
					if (loader == null)
					{
						loader = new Infragistics.Shared.SR();
					}
				}
			}
			return loader;
		}

		public static string GetString(string name)
		{
			return GetString(null, name);
		}

		public static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetString(null, name, args);
		}

		public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetString(culture, name, null);
		}

		public static string GetString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: true, culture, name, args);
		}

		private static string GetStringHelper(bool allowCustomizedStrings, CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			string text = null;
			if (allowCustomizedStrings)
			{
				text = global::ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer.GetCustomizedString(name);
			}
			if (text == null)
			{
				Infragistics.Shared.SR sR = GetLoader();
				if (sR == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				text = sR.GetString(name, culture);
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = string.Empty;
				}
			}
			if (args != null && args.Length != 0 && text.Length > 2)
			{
				try
				{
					text = string.Format(null, text, args);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		private string GetString(string name, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ResourceManager[] array = resources;
			foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager in array)
			{
				string text = resourceManager.GetString(name, culture);
				if (text != null)
				{
					return text;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(null, name);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(null, name, args);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetUncustomizedString(culture, name, null);
		}

		public static string GetUncustomizedString(CultureInfo culture, string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return GetStringHelper(allowCustomizedStrings: false, culture, name, args);
		}

		public static object GetObject(string name)
		{
			return GetObject(null, name);
		}

		public static object GetObject(CultureInfo culture, string name)
		{
			return GetLoader()?.GetObject(name, culture);
		}

		private object GetObject(string name, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			ResourceManager[] array = resources;
			foreach (ResourceManager resourceManager in array)
			{
				object obj = resourceManager.GetObject(name, culture);
				if (obj != null)
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
	internal sealed class LocalizedCategoryAttribute : CategoryAttribute
	{
		public LocalizedCategoryAttribute(string category)
			: base(category)
		{
		}

		protected override string GetLocalizedString(string value)
		{
			return Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, value);
		}
	}
	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.All, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
	internal sealed class LocalizedDescriptionAttribute : DescriptionAttribute
	{
		private bool loaded;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				if (!loaded)
				{
					loaded = true;
					base.DescriptionValue = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetUncustomizedString(null, base.Description);
				}
				return base.Description;
			}
		}

		public LocalizedDescriptionAttribute(string description)
			: base(description)
		{
		}
	}

	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property | AttributeTargets.Field, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
	internal sealed class LocalizedDisplayNameAttribute : DisplayNameAttribute
	{
		private bool loaded;

		private string resourceName;

		private string displayName;

		public override string DisplayName
		{
			get
			{
				if (!loaded)
				{
					loaded = true;
					displayName = Infragistics.Shared.SR.GetString(null, resourceName);
				}
				return displayName;
			}
		}

		public LocalizedDisplayNameAttribute(string resourceName)
		{
			this.resourceName = resourceName;
		}
	}
